AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
			BEGIN
				SET @pordTotalAmountStr = @pordTotalAmountStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			END
			SET @pordTotalAmountStr = @pordTotalAmountStr
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			SET @pordTotalAmountStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS BIGINT))),'')
			IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
			BEGIN
				SET @pordTotalAmountStr = @pordTotalAmountStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS BIGINT))),'')
			END
			SET @pordTotalAmountStr = REPLACE(@pordTotalAmountStr,'-',' ') 
		END
	END
	/**************************************************/
	/*Update*/
	UPDATE [dbo].[WSPreOrder]
	SET  [pordItemCount] = ISNULL(@pordItemCount,0)
		,[pordItemsAmount] = ISNULL(@pordItemsAmount,0)
		,[pordSumAmount] = ISNULL(@pordSumAmount,0)
		,[pordTax] = ISNULL(@pordTax,0)
		,[pordTotalAmount] = @pordTotalAmount
		,[pordTotalAmountStr] = @pordTotalAmountStr
		,[pordDiscount] = ISNULL(@pordDiscount,0)
		,[Month1] = @Month1
		/*AI14020325 - Add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
		,[pordItemsQty] = ISNULL(@SumQuantity,0)
		,[pordItemsDiscount] = ISNULL(@TotalDiscountSum,0)
		,[pordItemsTax] = ISNULL(@TotalTaxSum,0)
		/*AI14020325 - End*/
	WHERE PreOrderID = @PreOrderId
	/**/
	IF(@pordTotalAmount IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @LangId INT
		/**/
		DECLARE Crsr CURSOR FOR
			SELECT LanguageID
			FROM [dbo].[Languages]		
			WHERE LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
			AND ISNULL(IsActive,0) = 1
		/**/
		OPEN Crsr
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @LangId
			While @@Fetch_Status = 0
			BEGIN	 
				DECLARE @pordTotalAmountStrLang NVARCHAR(1000)	
				/*-------------------------------------------*/				
				IF(@LangId = 1065)
				BEGIN
					SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
					BEGIN
						SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = @pordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					END
					SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = @pordTotalAmountStrLang /*+ N' ����'*/
				END
				ELSE IF(@LangId = 1025)
				BEGIN
					SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
					BEGIN
						SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = @pordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					END
					SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = @pordTotalAmountStrLang
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS BIGINT))),'')
					IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
					BEGIN
						SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = @pordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS BIGINT))),'')
					END							
					SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = REPLACE(@pordTotalAmountStrLang,'-',' ')
				END
				/**/
				DECLARE @PreOrdIdLang INT
				SET @PreOrdIdLang = (SELECT [WSPreOrderLangID] FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder_Lang] WHERE PreOrderID = @PreOrderId AND LanguageID = @LangId)
				IF(@PreOrdIdLang IS NOT NULL)
				BEGIN
					UPDATE [dbo].[WSPreOrder_Lang]
						SET [pordTotalAmountStr] = @pordTotalAmountStrLang
					WHERE [WSPreOrderLangID] = @PreOrdIdLang						
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrder_Lang]
						([PreOrderID]
						,[LanguageID]
						,[pordTotalAmountStr])
					VALUES
						(@PreOrderId
						,@LangId
						,@pordTotalAmountStrLang)
				END
				/*-------------------------------------------*/
				FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @LangId
			END
		CLOSE Crsr
		DEALLOCATE Crsr	
	END							
	/*==================================================*/			
END		

/*==================================================*/	
/*RZ14030301- Clac and update Amount in currencyI*/
EXEC [dbo].[MobspCalcAndUpdatePreOrderAmountsCurrency]	
@PreOrderId = @PreOrderId,
@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID
/*RZ14030301- End*/
/*==================================================*/
/**/
END








GO
-- [dbo].[MobspCalcAndUpdatePreOrderAmountsCurrency]





/*RZ14030301*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[MobspCalcAndUpdatePreOrderAmountsCurrency]
/* 	
	exec MobspCalcAndUpdatePreOrderAmountsCurrency 4841,1033,1065	
*/
(
@PreOrderId INT,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
DECLARE @pordItemsAmount NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@pordInstallAmount NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
@pordDiscount NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@pordSumAmount NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@pordTax NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@pordTotalAmount NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,		
@pordTransportation NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@pordRound NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,	
@pordCurrencyUnitId INT = NULL,	@pordItemsDiscount NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@pordItemsTax NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,	@pordCurrencyUnitIdI INT = NULL,
@pordCurrencyUnitIRate FLOAT = NULL,@pordItemsAmountCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@pordInstallAmountCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@pordDiscountCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@pordSumAmountCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@pordTaxCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@pordTotalAmountCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@pordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@pordTransportationCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@pordRoundCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@pordItemsDiscountCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@pordItemsTaxCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@CurrencyUnitIsDecimal BIT = NULL,@IsDivisionCurrencyRate BIT = NULL,@RialCurrencyUnitId INT = NULL
/*-------------------------------------*/
SELECT TOP (1)     
     @pordItemsAmount = [pordItemsAmount]
    ,@pordInstallAmount = [pordInstallAmount]
    ,@pordDiscount = [pordDiscount]
    ,@pordSumAmount = [pordSumAmount]
    ,@pordTax = [pordTax]    
    ,@pordTotalAmount = [pordTotalAmount]        
    ,@pordTransportation = [pordTransportation]
    ,@pordRound = [pordRound]    
    ,@pordCurrencyUnitId = [pordCurrencyUnitId]    
    ,@pordItemsDiscount = [pordItemsDiscount]
    ,@pordItemsTax = [pordItemsTax]    
    ,@pordCurrencyUnitIdI = [pordCurrencyUnitIdI]
    ,@pordCurrencyUnitIRate = [pordCurrencyUnitIRate]    
FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder] PO
WHERE [PreOrderID] = @PreOrderId
/*-----------------------------------*/
SET @CurrencyUnitIsDecimal = (SELECT TOP 1 [IsDecimal]
							  FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnits] 
							  WHERE [CurrencyUnitID] = @pordCurrencyUnitIdI)
SET @RialCurrencyUnitId = (SELECT TOP 1 [CurrencyUnitID]            
						   FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnits]
						   WHERE (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST([CurrencyUnit] AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
								  NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) 
								= (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(N'����' AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
								   NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) 
							)

IF(ISNULL(@DefaultLanguageID,0) = 0)
BEGIN
	SET @DefaultLanguageID = (SELECT TOP(1) [DefaultLanguageID] FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting])
END
/*-----------------------------------*/
IF(ISNULL(@pordCurrencyUnitId,0) <> 0 AND ISNULL(@pordCurrencyUnitIdI,0) <> 0 AND ISNULL(@pordCurrencyUnitIRate,0) <> 0)
BEGIN
/**/
	IF(ISNULL(@pordCurrencyUnitId,0) <> ISNULL(@pordCurrencyUnitIdI,0))
	BEGIN
	/**/	
		/*--------------------------------------------------*/
		IF(ISNULL(@RialCurrencyUnitId,0) = ISNULL(@pordCurrencyUnitId,0))
		BEGIN
		/**/
			/*Change rate when main currency unit is rial*/
			SET @pordCurrencyUnitIRate = CAST((1.0/ISNULL(@pordCurrencyUnitIRate,1)) AS FLOAT)
		/**/
		END		
		/*--------------------------------------------------*/			
		IF(ISNULL(@pordItemsAmount,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @pordItemsAmountCurrencyI = ISNULL(@pordItemsAmount,0) * ISNULL(@pordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@pordInstallAmount,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @pordInstallAmountCurrencyI = ISNULL(@pordInstallAmount,0) * ISNULL(@pordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@pordDiscount,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @pordDiscountCurrencyI = ISNULL(@pordDiscount,0) * ISNULL(@pordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@pordSumAmount,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @pordSumAmountCurrencyI = ISNULL(@pordSumAmount,0) * ISNULL(@pordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@pordTax,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @pordTaxCurrencyI = ISNULL(@pordTax,0) * ISNULL(@pordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@pordTotalAmount,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @pordTotalAmountCurrencyI = ISNULL(@pordTotalAmount,0) * ISNULL(@pordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@pordTransportation,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @pordTransportationCurrencyI = ISNULL(@pordTransportation,0) * ISNULL(@pordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@pordRound,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @pordRoundCurrencyI = ISNULL(@pordRound,0) * ISNULL(@pordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@pordItemsDiscount,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @pordItemsDiscountCurrencyI = ISNULL(@pordItemsDiscount,0) * ISNULL(@pordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@pordItemsTax,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @pordItemsTaxCurrencyI = ISNULL(@pordItemsTax,0) * ISNULL(@pordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		/*--------------------------------------------------*/
		IF(ISNULL(@CurrencyUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
		BEGIN	
		/**/
			SET @pordItemsAmountCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordItemsAmountCurrencyI))					
			SET @pordInstallAmountCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordInstallAmountCurrencyI))					
			SET @pordDiscountCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordDiscountCurrencyI))					
			SET @pordSumAmountCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordSumAmountCurrencyI))					
			SET @pordTaxCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordTaxCurrencyI))					
			SET @pordTotalAmountCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordTotalAmountCurrencyI))					
			SET @pordTransportationCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordTransportationCurrencyI))					
			SET @pordRoundCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordRoundCurrencyI))	
			SET @pordItemsDiscountCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordItemsDiscountCurrencyI))					
			SET @pordItemsTaxCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordItemsTaxCurrencyI))								
		/**/
		END
		/*--------------------------------------------------*/
	/**/
	END
/**/
END
/*-----------------------------------*/
/*************** TotalAmountStr *******************/
DECLARE @TotalAmoutI NVARCHAR(MAX),@TotalAmoutII NVARCHAR(MAX)
IF(@pordTotalAmountCurrencyI IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN			
	SET @TotalAmoutI = SUBSTRING(CAST(@pordTotalAmountCurrencyI AS NVARCHAR(MAX)), 1, CHARINDEX('.',CAST(@pordTotalAmountCurrencyI AS NVARCHAR(MAX)))-1)                                    
	SET @TotalAmoutII = SUBSTRING(CAST(@pordTotalAmountCurrencyI AS NVARCHAR(MAX)), CHARINDEX('.', CAST(@pordTotalAmountCurrencyI AS NVARCHAR(MAX)))+1, 2)
	/**/
	IF(@DefaultLanguageID = 1065)
	BEGIN
		SET @pordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
		IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
		BEGIN
			SET @pordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr = @pordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
		END
		SET @pordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr = @pordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr 
	END
	ELSE IF(@DefaultLanguageID = 1025)
	BEGIN
		SET @pordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
		IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
		BEGIN
			SET @pordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr = @pordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
		END
		SET @pordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr = @pordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		SET @pordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS BIGINT))),'')
		IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
		BEGIN
			SET @pordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr = @pordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS BIGINT))),'')
		END
		SET @pordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr = REPLACE(@pordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr,'-',' ') 
	END
END
/*-----------------------------------*/
UPDATE [dbo].[WSPreOrder]
SET  [pordItemsAmountCurrencyI] = @pordItemsAmountCurrencyI
    ,[pordInstallAmountCurrencyI] = @pordInstallAmountCurrencyI
    ,[pordDiscountCurrencyI] = @pordDiscountCurrencyI
    ,[pordSumAmountCurrencyI] = @pordSumAmountCurrencyI
    ,[pordTaxCurrencyI] = @pordTaxCurrencyI
    ,[pordTotalAmountCurrencyI] = @pordTotalAmountCurrencyI
    ,[pordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr] = @pordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr
    ,[pordTransportationCurrencyI] = @pordTransportationCurrencyI
    ,[pordRoundCurrencyI] = @pordRoundCurrencyI
    ,[pordItemsDiscountCurrencyI] = @pordItemsDiscountCurrencyI
    ,[pordItemsTaxCurrencyI] = @pordItemsTaxCurrencyI
WHERE [PreOrderID] = @PreOrderId
/*-----------------------------------*/
/*************** TotalAmountStr in other language *******************/
IF(@pordTotalAmountCurrencyI IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	DECLARE @LangId INT
	/**/
	DECLARE Crsr CURSOR LOCAL FOR
		SELECT LanguageID
		FROM [dbo].[Languages]		
		WHERE LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
		AND ISNULL(IsActive,0) = 1
	/**/
	OPEN Crsr
	FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @LangId
		While @@Fetch_Status = 0
		BEGIN	 
			DECLARE @pordTotalAmountStrLang NVARCHAR(1000)	
			/*-------------------------------------------*/				
			IF(@LangId = 1065)
			BEGIN
				SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
				IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
				BEGIN
					SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = @pordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
				END
				SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = @pordTotalAmountStrLang /*+ N' ����'*/
			END
			ELSE IF(@LangId = 1025)
			BEGIN
				SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
				IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
				BEGIN
					SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = @pordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
				END
				SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = @pordTotalAmountStrLang
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS BIGINT))),'')
				IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
				BEGIN
					SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = @pordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS BIGINT))),'')
				END							
				SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = REPLACE(@pordTotalAmountStrLang,'-',' ')
			END
			/**/
			DECLARE @PreOrdIdLang INT
			SET @PreOrdIdLang = (SELECT TOP(1) [WSPreOrderLangID] FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder_Lang] WHERE PreOrderID = @PreOrderId AND LanguageID = @LangId)
			IF(@PreOrdIdLang IS NOT NULL)
			BEGIN
				UPDATE [dbo].[WSPreOrder_Lang]
					SET [pordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr] = @pordTotalAmountStrLang
				WHERE [WSPreOrderLangID] = @PreOrdIdLang						
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrder_Lang]
					([PreOrderID]
					,[LanguageID]
					,[pordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr])
				VALUES
					(@PreOrderId
					,@LangId
					,@pordTotalAmountStrLang)
			END
			/*-------------------------------------------*/
			FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @LangId
		END
	CLOSE Crsr
	DEALLOCATE Crsr	
END
/*-----------------------------------*/
/*------------ Update Items Amount in Currency -----------*/
EXEC [dbo].[MobspCalcAndUpdatePreOrderItemAmountsCurrency]
@PreOrderId = @PreOrderId,
@PreOrderItemIds = ''
/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/**/
END









GO
-- [dbo].[MobspCalcAndUpdatePreOrderItemsTax]





 



/*RZ14021030*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[MobspCalcAndUpdatePreOrderItemsTax]
/* 	
	declare @Result INT = 0
	exec MobspCalcAndUpdatePreOrderItemsTax 	
	@PreOrderId = 42842,
	@Month1 = 10,
	@Result = @Result OUTPUT
	select @Result 
*/
(
@PreOrderId INT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@Result INT OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*--------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*------------------------ Define variables --------------------*/
DECLARE @TspPrdSendMethod INT = NULL
,@TspSettingId INT = NULL,@TspIdentify NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,@TspPrivateKey NVARCHAR(20) = NULL
,@cuUnitIsDecimal BIT = NULL
/*------------------------ Set variables ----------------------*/
SET @TspPrdSendMethod = (SELECT TOP(1) [TspPrdSendMethod] FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting])

SELECT TOP (1) 
	 @TspSettingId = [TspSettingId]
	,@TspIdentify = [TspIdentify]
    ,@TspPrivateKey = [TspPrivateKey]      
FROM [dbo].[TspSetting]

SET @cuUnitIsDecimal = (SELECT TOP 1 [IsDecimal]
						FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnits] 
						WHERE [CurrencyUnitID] = (SELECT pordCurrencyUnitId 
												  FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder] 
												  WHERE PreOrderID = @PreOrderId))

/*-------------------------------------------------------------*/
IF(ISNULL(@TspSettingId,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@TspIdentify,'') <> '' AND ISNULL(@TspPrivateKey,'') <> '')
BEGIN
/**/
	/*-----------------------------------------------------------*/
	/*----------------------- Declare table temp ----------------*/
	DECLARE @Tmp_PordItemSumTax TABLE(		
		[pordITaxSum] NUMERIC(18,2),
		[TspGroupIndentify] NVARCHAR(100)
	)
	DECLARE @Tmp_PordItems TABLE(		
		 [PreOrderItemID] INT	
		,[pordIPrdId] INT				
		,[pordITaxIndex] NUMERIC(18,2)			   
		,[pordIPriceSum] NUMERIC(18,2)	        
        ,[pordITaxSum] NUMERIC(18,2)	
		,[TspPrdIndentify] NVARCHAR(100)
		,[TspFGIndentify] NVARCHAR(100)
		,[TspSGIndentify] NVARCHAR(100)		
	)
	/*------------------- Fill table temp -----------------------*/
	INSERT INTO @Tmp_PordItems([PreOrderItemID],[pordIPrdId],[pordITaxIndex],[pordIPriceSum],[pordITaxSum],[TspPrdIndentify],[TspFGIndentify],[TspSGIndentify])
	SELECT 
		 [PreOrderItemID]
		,[pordIPrdId]
		,[pordITaxIndex]		
		,[pordIPriceSum]
        ,[pordITaxSum]
		,(SELECT TOP(1) [TspPrdIndentify] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] P WHERE P.ProductID = POI.pordIPrdId) [TspPrdIndentify]
		,(SELECT [TspFGIndentify] FROM [dbo].[WSProductFirstGroup] WHERE PrdFirstGroupId = 
			(SELECT FirstGroupId FROM [dbo].[WSProductSecondGroup] WHERE PrdSecondGroupId = 
				(SELECT PrdGroupId FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] p WHERE ProductID = pordIPrdId))) [TspFGIndentify]
		,(SELECT [TspSGIndentify] FROM [dbo].[WSProductSecondGroup] WHERE PrdSecondGroupId = 
			(SELECT PrdGroupId FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] p WHERE ProductID = pordIPrdId)) [TspSGIndentify]		
	FROM dbo.WSPreOrderItems POI
	WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderId
	AND ISNULL(pordINotInSum,0) = 0
	AND ISNULL([pordITaxSum],0) > 0
	ORDER BY pordIOrderView
	/*-----------------------------------------------------------*/
	IF(ISNULL(@TspPrdSendMethod,0) = 1)/*Product first group*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		INSERT INTO @Tmp_PordItemSumTax([pordITaxSum],[TspGroupIndentify])
		SELECT 			 
			 [pordITaxSum]
			,LTRIM(RTRIM([TspFGIndentify])) 
		FROM @Tmp_PordItems
		WHERE ISNULL(LTRIM(RTRIM([TspFGIndentify])),'') <> ''		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(ISNULL(@TspPrdSendMethod,0) = 3)/*Product second group*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		INSERT INTO @Tmp_PordItemSumTax([pordITaxSum],[TspGroupIndentify])
		SELECT 			 
			 [pordITaxSum]
			,LTRIM(RTRIM([TspSGIndentify])) 
		FROM @Tmp_PordItems
		WHERE ISNULL(LTRIM(RTRIM([TspSGIndentify])),'') <> ''	
	/**/
	END
	/*-----------------------------------------------------------*/
	BEGIN TRY
	BEGIN TRANSACTION /*CalcPordITax*/
	/**/
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		DECLARE @PreOrderItemID INT = NULL,@TspGroupIndentify NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,@PrdGroupPordITaxSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
		@SumPordITaxSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@DiffPordITaxSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
		@SumPordIPriceSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@SumPordITaxIndex NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@NewPordITaxIndex NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@PordICountInGroup INT = NULL,
		@CalcTxt BIT = NULL
		
		DECLARE Crsr_CalcPordITax CURSOR LOCAL FOR				
			SELECT DISTINCT
				[TspGroupIndentify]
			FROM @Tmp_PordItemSumTax
		OPEN Crsr_CalcPordITax
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_CalcPordITax INTO @TspGroupIndentify
		WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
		BEGIN
			/*-----------------------------------------------*/
			SET @PreOrderItemID = NULL
			SET @PrdGroupPordITaxSum = NULL
			SET @SumPordITaxSum = NULL
			SET @DiffpordITaxSum = NULL
			SET @SumPordIPriceSum = NULL
			SET @SumPordITaxIndex = NULL
			SET @NewPordITaxIndex = NULL
			SET @PordICountInGroup = NULL
			SET @TspGroupIndentify = LTRIM(RTRIM(@TspGroupIndentify))
			SET @CalcTxt = NULL
			/*-----------------------------------------------*/
			SET @PrdGroupPordITaxSum = ISNULL((SELECT SUM(ISNULL([pordITaxSum],0)) 
											  FROM @Tmp_PordItemSumTax 
											  WHERE LTRIM(RTRIM([TspGroupIndentify])) = @TspGroupIndentify),0)

			IF(ISNULL(@TspPrdSendMethod,0) = 1)/*Product first group*/
			BEGIN
			/**/				
				SELECT 
					@SumPordIPriceSum = SUM(ISNULL([pordIPriceSum],0)),
					@SumPordITaxIndex = SUM(ISNULL([pordITaxIndex],0))
				FROM @Tmp_PordItems
				WHERE ISNULL(LTRIM(RTRIM([TspFGIndentify])),'') = @TspGroupIndentify

				SET @PordICountInGroup = ISNULL((SELECT COUNT(*)
											    FROM @Tmp_PordItemSumTax 
											    WHERE LTRIM(RTRIM([TspGroupIndentify])) = @TspGroupIndentify),0)

				IF((SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT [pordITaxIndex]) FROM   @Tmp_PordItems WHERE ISNULL(LTRIM(RTRIM([TspFGIndentify])),'') = @TspGroupIndentify) = 1)
				BEGIN
					SET @CalcTxt = 1
				END

				IF(ISNULL(@PordICountInGroup,0) > 0)
				BEGIN
					SET @NewPordITaxIndex =  ISNULL(@SumPordITaxIndex,0) / @PordICountInGroup					
				END
				
				IF(ISNULL(@SumPordIPriceSum,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@NewPordITaxIndex,0) > 0)
				BEGIN
					SET @SumPordITaxSum = (ISNULL(@SumPordIPriceSum,0) * ISNULL(@NewPordITaxIndex,0)) / 100					
				END				
				SET @PreOrderItemID = (SELECT TOP(1) [PreOrderItemID]
									FROM @Tmp_PordItems
									WHERE ISNULL(LTRIM(RTRIM([TspFGIndentify])),'') = @TspGroupIndentify)
			/**/
			END
			ELSE IF(ISNULL(@TspPrdSendMethod,0) = 3)/*Product second group*/
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SELECT 
					@SumPordIPriceSum = SUM(ISNULL([pordIPriceSum],0)),
					@SumPordITaxIndex = SUM(ISNULL([pordITaxIndex],0))
				FROM @Tmp_PordItems
				WHERE ISNULL(LTRIM(RTRIM([TspSGIndentify])),'') = @TspGroupIndentify

				SET @PordICountInGroup = ISNULL((SELECT COUNT(*)
											    FROM @Tmp_PordItemSumTax 
											    WHERE LTRIM(RTRIM([TspGroupIndentify])) = @TspGroupIndentify),0)
				
				IF((SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT [pordITaxIndex]) FROM   @Tmp_PordItems WHERE ISNULL(LTRIM(RTRIM([TspSGIndentify])),'') = @TspGroupIndentify) = 1)
				BEGIN
					SET @CalcTxt = 1
				END

				IF(ISNULL(@PordICountInGroup,0) > 0)
				BEGIN
					SET @NewPordITaxIndex =  ISNULL(@SumPordITaxIndex,0) / @PordICountInGroup					
				END
				
				IF(ISNULL(@SumPordIPriceSum,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@NewPordITaxIndex,0) > 0)
				BEGIN
					SET @SumPordITaxSum = (ISNULL(@SumPordIPriceSum,0) * ISNULL(@NewPordITaxIndex,0)) / 100
					
				END

				SET @PreOrderItemID = (SELECT TOP(1) [PreOrderItemID]
									FROM @Tmp_PordItems
									WHERE ISNULL(LTRIM(RTRIM([TspSGIndentify])),'') = @TspGroupIndentify)
			/**/
			END
			/**/
			IF(ISNULL(@cuUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
			BEGIN
				SET @SumPordITaxSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@SumPordITaxSum))									
			END
			/*-----------------------------------------------*/			
			IF(ISNULL(@SumPordITaxSum,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@PrdGroupPordITaxSum,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@PreOrderItemID,0) <> 0 AND ISNULL(@CalcTxt,0) = 1)
			BEGIN
			/**/				
				SET @DiffPordITaxSum = ISNULL(@SumPordITaxSum,0) - ISNULL(@PrdGroupPordITaxSum,0)				
				IF(ISNULL(@DiffPordITaxSum,0) <> 0 )
				BEGIN
				/**/
					/*---------------------------------------------*/
					DECLARE @pordIQuantity FLOAT = NULL,@pordITotalPrice NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
					@pordIFCR FLOAT = NULL, @pordITax NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@pordITaxIndex NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
					@pordIFCRI FLOAT = NULL,@pordIPriceSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@pordITaxSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
					@pordIQuantityTotal FLOAT = NULL,@PrdQtyNoFCR BIT = NULL,@TmpI NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL
					/*---------------------------------------------*/
					SELECT TOP (1) 
						 @pordIQuantity = [pordIQuantity]						
						,@pordITotalPrice = [pordITotalPrice]												
						,@pordIFCR = [pordIFCR]
						,@pordITax = [pordITax]
						,@pordITaxIndex = [pordITaxIndex]						
						,@pordIFCRI = [pordIFCRI]						
						,@pordIPriceSum = [pordIPriceSum]						
						,@pordITaxSum = [pordITaxSum]
						,@pordIQuantityTotal = [pordIQuantityTotal]
						,@PrdQtyNoFCR = (SELECT TOP(1) [PrdQtyNoFCR] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] WHERE ProductId = pordIPrdId)	
					FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems] 
					WHERE [PreOrderItemID] = @PreOrderItemID
					/*---------------------------------------------*/
					SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@pordIQuantity,1) * ISNULL(@pordIFCR,1) * ISNULL(@pordIFCRI,1)
					IF(ISNULL(@PrdQtyNoFCR,0) = 1)
					BEGIN
					/**/
						SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@pordIQuantity,1)
					/**/
					END
					IF(ISNULL(@pordIQuantityTotal,0) > 0)
					BEGIN
					/**/
						SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@pordIQuantityTotal,1) * ISNULL(@pordIFCRI,1)
						IF(ISNULL(@PrdQtyNoFCR,0) = 1)
						BEGIN
						/**/
							SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@pordIQuantityTotal,1)
						/**/
						END
					/**/
					END
					/*------------------------------------*/
					/*----------------- Calculate -----------------*/
					SET @pordITaxSum = ISNULL(@pordITaxSum,0) + ISNULL(@DiffPordITaxSum,0)					
					IF(ISNULL(@pordITaxSum,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@pordIPriceSum,0) > 0)
					BEGIN
						SET @pordITaxIndex = (ISNULL(@pordITaxSum,0) * 100) / ISNULL(@pordIPriceSum,0)
						SET @pordITax = (ISNULL(@pordITaxSum,0) / ISNULL(@TmpI,0))
					END
					SET @pordITotalPrice = ISNULL(@pordIPriceSum,0) + ISNULL(@pordITaxSum,0)
					/*--------- Calculate item by currency unit ------*/
					IF(ISNULL(@cuUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
					BEGIN
						SET @pordITaxSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordITaxSum))
						SET @pordITax = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordITax))									
						SET @pordITotalPrice = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordITotalPrice))						
					END
					/*--------------------- Update item --------------*/					
					UPDATE [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems]
					SET  [pordITotalPrice] = @pordITotalPrice
						,[pordITax] = @pordITax
						,[pordITaxIndex] = @pordITaxIndex
						,[pordITaxSum] = @pordITaxSum			
						,[Month1] = @Month1						
					WHERE PreOrderItemID = @PreOrderItemID	
					/*------------------------------------------------*/
				/**/
				END							   
			/**/
			END			
			/*-----------------------------------------------*/
			FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_CalcPordITax INTO @TspGroupIndentify
		END
		CLOSE Crsr_CalcPordITax
		DEALLOCATE Crsr_CalcPordITax		
		/*-----------------------------------------------------------------*/	
		/*-----------------------------------------------------------------*/		
		COMMIT /*CalcPordITax*/
		SET @Result = 1
		RETURN @Result
	END TRY
	BEGIN CATCH
		IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
			ROLLBACK /*CalcPordITax*/	
		/**/
		SET @Result = -1
		CLOSE Crsr_CalcPordITax
		DEALLOCATE Crsr_CalcPordITax
		/**/		
	END CATCH	   
	/*-----------------------------------------------------------*/
/**/
END
/*--------------------------------------------------------------*/
/**/
/**/
END












GO
-- [dbo].[MobspCheckActionForUser]





/*AI14020529*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspCheckActionForUser]
/*
	DECLARE @HasAction BIT
	EXEC MobspCheckActionForUser N'frmPreOrderDataEntry', N'actAutoCalcNumRef', 3, @HasAction OUTPUT
	SELECT @HasAction
*/
(
@FormName NVARCHAR(50),
@ActionName NVARCHAR(50),
@UserId INT,
@HasAction BIT OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
	IF EXISTS (
		SELECT * FROM dbo.CrmsysRollPermission
		WHERE FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = @FormName )
		AND FormActionId = (SELECT Id FROM dbo.CrmsysFormActions WHERE ActionName = @ActionName
							AND FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = @FormName ))
		AND RollId = (SELECT TOP 1 RollId FROM dbo.UsersPermission WHERE UserId = @UserId AND PrjId = 11/*CRM*/))
	BEGIN
		SET @HasAction = 1
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		SET @HasAction = 0
	END
END







GO
-- [dbo].[MobspCheckCmpDataPermissionForUser]




/*RZ971221*/
/*AI990313- add user site*/
CREATE Procedure [dbo].[MobspCheckCmpDataPermissionForUser]
(
/*  
	exec MobspCheckCmpDataPermissionForUser 		
*/
@Mode INT,
@Id INT,
@CrmUserId INT,
@IsAccess BIT OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
	DECLARE @CheckForbidden BIT = 0, @CheckJustAccess BIT = 1
	IF(@CrmUserId= -10) /*AI990313 user is site*/
	BEGIN
		SET @IsAccess = 1
	END
	ELSE
	IF(@Mode = 1)/*Province*/
	BEGIN
		/**/
		/*Forbidden*/
		IF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenProvince
			WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF ((SELECT TOP 1 [ForbiddenProvinceId] FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenProvince
				WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId
				AND [ForbiddenProvinceId] = @Id) IS NOT NULL)
				SET @CheckForbidden = 1 
			ELSE
				SET @CheckForbidden = 0
		END
		/*JustAccess*/
		IF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessProvince
			WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId ) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF((SELECT TOP 1 [JustAccessProvinceId]     
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyJustAccessProvince]
				WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId
				AND [JustAccessProvinceId] = @Id) IS NULL)
				SET @CheckJustAccess = 0
			ELSE
				SET @CheckJustAccess = 1
		END
		/*Set Access*/
		IF(@CheckForbidden = 0 AND @CheckJustAccess = 1)
			SET @IsAccess = 1		
		/**/
	END
	/*---------------------------------*/
	ELSE IF(@Mode = 2)/*City*/
	BEGIN
		/**/
		/*Forbidden*/
		IF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenCity
			WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF ((SELECT TOP 1 ForbiddenCityId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenCity
				WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId
				AND [ForbiddenCityId] = @Id) IS NOT NULL)
				SET @CheckForbidden = 1 
			ELSE
				SET @CheckForbidden = 0
		END
		/*JustAccess*/
		IF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessCity
			WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId ) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF((SELECT TOP 1 [JustAccessCityId]     
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyJustAccessCity]
				WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId
				AND [JustAccessCityId] = @Id) IS NULL)
				SET @CheckJustAccess = 0
			ELSE
				SET @CheckJustAccess = 1
		END
		/*Set Access*/
		IF(@CheckForbidden = 0 AND @CheckJustAccess = 1)
			SET @IsAccess = 1		
		/**/
	END
	/*---------------------------------*/
	ELSE IF(@Mode = 3)/*Group*/
	BEGIN
		/**/
		/*Forbidden*/
		IF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenGroup
			WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF ((SELECT TOP 1 ForbiddenGroupId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenGroup
				WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId
				AND ForbiddenGroupId = @Id) IS NOT NULL)
				SET @CheckForbidden = 1 
			ELSE
				SET @CheckForbidden = 0
		END
		/*JustAccess*/
		IF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessGroup
			WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId ) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF((SELECT TOP 1 [JustAccessGroupId]     
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyJustAccessGroup]
				WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId
				AND [JustAccessGroupId] = @Id) IS NULL)
				SET @CheckJustAccess = 0
			ELSE
				SET @CheckJustAccess = 1
		END
		/*Set Access*/
		IF(@CheckForbidden = 0 AND @CheckJustAccess = 1)
			SET @IsAccess = 1		
		/**/
	END
	/*---------------------------------*/
	ELSE IF(@Mode = 4)/*SubGroup*/
	BEGIN
		/**/
		/*Forbidden*/
		IF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenSubGroup
			WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF ((SELECT TOP 1 ForbiddenSubGroupId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenSubGroup
				WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId
				AND ForbiddenSubGroupId = @Id) IS NOT NULL)
				SET @CheckForbidden = 1 
			ELSE
				SET @CheckForbidden = 0
		END
		/*JustAccess*/
		IF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessSubGroup
			WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId ) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF((SELECT TOP 1 [JustAccessSubGroupId]     
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyJustAccessSubGroup]
				WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId
				AND [JustAccessSubGroupId] = @Id) IS NULL)
				SET @CheckJustAccess = 0
			ELSE
				SET @CheckJustAccess = 1
		END
		/*Set Access*/
		IF(@CheckForbidden = 0 AND @CheckJustAccess = 1)
			SET @IsAccess = 1		
		/**/
	END
	/*---------------------------------*/
	ELSE IF(@Mode = 5)/*GroupIV*/
	BEGIN
		/**/
		/*Forbidden*/
		IF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenGroupIV
			WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF ((SELECT TOP 1 ForbiddenGroupIVId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenGroupIV
				WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId
				AND ForbiddenGroupIVId = @Id) IS NOT NULL)
				SET @CheckForbidden = 1 
			ELSE
				SET @CheckForbidden = 0
		END
		/*JustAccess*/
		IF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessGroupIV
			WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId ) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF((SELECT TOP 1 [JustAccessGroupIVId]     
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyJustAccessGroupIV]
				WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId
				AND [JustAccessGroupIVId] = @Id) IS NULL)
				SET @CheckJustAccess = 0
			ELSE
				SET @CheckJustAccess = 1
		END
		/*Set Access*/
		IF(@CheckForbidden = 0 AND @CheckJustAccess = 1)
			SET @IsAccess = 1		
		/**/
	END
	/*---------------------------------*/
	ELSE IF(@Mode = 6)/*Company*/
	BEGIN
		/**/
		/*Forbidden*/
		IF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenCompany
			WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF ((SELECT TOP 1 ForbiddenCompanyId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenCompany
				WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId
				AND ForbiddenCompanyId = @Id) IS NOT NULL)
				SET @CheckForbidden = 1 
			ELSE
				SET @CheckForbidden = 0
		END
		/*JustAccess*/
		IF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessCompany
			WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId ) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF((SELECT TOP 1 [JustAccessCompanyId]     
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyJustAccessCompany]
				WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId
				AND [JustAccessCompanyId] = @Id) IS NULL)
				SET @CheckJustAccess = 0
			ELSE
				SET @CheckJustAccess = 1
		END
		/*Set Access*/
		IF(@CheckForbidden = 0 AND @CheckJustAccess = 1)
			SET @IsAccess = 1		
		/**/
	END
	/*---------------------------------*/
	ELSE IF(@Mode = 7)/*cmpReferUser*/
	BEGIN
		/**/
		
		/*Set Access*/
		IF(
			(SELECT * FROM
			(SELECT @CrmUserId ReferUserId 
			 UNION 
			 SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers 
			 WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId 
			 AND LinkedTypeId = 14 ) as tmp
			 WHERE ReferUserId = @Id			 			 
			 ) IS NOT NULL		
		  )
			SET @IsAccess = 1		
		/**/
	END
	/*---------------------------------*/
	ELSE IF(@Mode = 8)/*Access see company with all permission*/
	BEGIN
		/**/
		DECLARE @Qry NVARCHAR(MAX) = '' , @cmpDataPermision VARCHAR(max) = '', @CmpId INT		
		/*SET DataPermission*/		 
		EXEC dbo.MobspSetcmpDataPermission @CrmUserId
		SELECT @cmpDataPermision = TXT
		FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
		WHERE TypeId = 20
		AND UserId = @CrmUserId
		/*SET Query*/
		SET @Qry = 'SELECT @CmpId = [CompanyID] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] C 
					WHERE CompanyID = ' + CAST(@Id AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + ' ' + ISNULL(@cmpDataPermision,'')		
		/*RUN Query*/
        EXECUTE sp_executesql @Qry, N' @CmpId INT OUTPUT', @CmpId OUTPUT			
		/*Set Access*/
		IF(@CmpId IS NOT NULL)
			SET @IsAccess = 1		
		/**/
	END
/**/
END




GO

/*AI14020529 - Copied from web*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicateDocNumRef]
(		
/*
	EXEC MobspCheckDuplicateDocNumRef 'dddd', 0
*/
@DocNumRef NVARCHAR(150),
@CurrDocId INT,
@DocOprId INT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
	SELECT DocNumber,DocNumRef
	FROM dbo.WSDoc
	WHERE DocNumRef = @DocNumRef
	AND DocID <> @CurrDocId	
	AND OprId = @DocOprId
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicatePrdSerial]






/*AI14020528 - Copied from web*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicatePrdSerial]
/* 
	EXEC MobspCheckDuplicatePrdSerial  1, 2, 1, 7, '100',13, 1033, 1065, 0
*/		
(
@OprId INT, 
@FPId INT,
@WHID INT,
@PrdId INT,
@Serial NVARCHAR(50),
@CurrId INT,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL,
@DocItemSerialID INT = NULL
)
As
BEGIN
/**/
IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	SELECT 
		DIS.DocItemSerialID, 
		DIS.ItemId, 
		DIS.Serial, 
		DIS.SerialId, 
		DIS.StatusId,
		DI.PrdId,
		(SELECT PrdName FROM dbo.WSProducts WHERE ProductID = DI.PrdId) PrdName,
        D.DocNumber 
	FROM dbo.WSDocItemSerials DIS 
	INNER JOIN dbo.WSDocItems DI ON(DIS.ItemId = DI.DocItemID)
	INNER JOIN dbo.WSDoc D ON(DI.DocId = D.DocID)
	WHERE OprId = @OprId
	AND DI.PrdId = @PrdId
	AND DIS.Serial = @Serial
	AND DIS.WHId = @WHId
	AND DIS.DocItemSerialID <> @CurrId
	AND ISNULL(SerialId, 0) = Isnull(@DocItemSerialID, 0)
END
ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	SELECT 
		DIS.DocItemSerialID, 
		DIS.ItemId, 
		DIS.Serial, 
		DIS.SerialId, 
		DIS.StatusId,
		DI.PrdId,
		(SELECT PrdName FROM dbo.WSProducts_Lang PLN WHERE PLN.ProductID = DI.PrdId AND PLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID) PrdName,
        D.DocNumber 
	FROM dbo.WSDocItemSerials DIS 
	INNER JOIN dbo.WSDocItems DI ON(DIS.ItemId = DI.DocItemID)
	INNER JOIN dbo.WSDoc D ON(DI.DocId = D.DocID)
	WHERE OprId = @OprId
	AND DI.PrdId = @PrdId
	AND DIS.Serial = @Serial
	AND DIS.WHId = @WHId
	AND DIS.DocItemSerialID <> @CurrId
	AND Isnull(SerialId, 0) = Isnull(@DocItemSerialID, 0)
END
/**/
END







GO
-- [dbo].[MobspCheckHaveCmpProjectByOrdIssuePrdId]






/*AI14020715 - Copied from web*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspCheckHaveCmpProjectByOrdIssuePrdId]
/* 
	EXEC MobspCheckHaveCmpProjectByOrdIssuePrdId  6156,10571,1033,1065
*/	
(
@CompanyId INT,
@ProductId INT ,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL 
)	
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*----------------------------------------------------*/
DECLARE @ProjectId INT = NULL,
@PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleGroupId INT = NULL,@PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleId INT = NULL,@PrdOrdIssuePrjPostFix NVARCHAR(200) = NULL,
@PrdOrdIssuePrjUsedType INT = NULL,
/**/
@prjTitle NVARCHAR(100) = NULL, @prjPostFix NVARCHAR(200) = NULL, @prjTitleGroup NVARCHAR(200) = NULL
/*------------- Set variables ------------*/
SELECT TOP (1) 	   
	 @PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleGroupId = [PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleGroupId]
	,@PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleId = [PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleId]
	,@PrdOrdIssuePrjPostFix = [PrdOrdIssuePrjPostFix]
	,@PrdOrdIssuePrjUsedType = [PrdOrdIssuePrjUsedType]			
FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]
WHERE [ProductID] = @ProductId
/*---------------------------*/
SET @prjTitle = (SELECT TOP(1) [ProjectTitle] FROM dbo.CrmProjectTitles PT WHERE [ProjectTitleID] = @PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleId)
SET @prjTitleGroup = (SELECT TOP(1) [ProjectTitleGroup] FROM dbo.[CrmProjectTitleGroups] PT WHERE [ProjectTitleGroupId] = @PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleGroupId)
SET @prjPostFix = @PrdOrdIssuePrjPostFix
/*----------------------------*/
SET @ProjectId = (SELECT TOP 1 P.ProjectID 
						FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] P 
						WHERE P.prjCompanyId = @CompanyId 
						AND ISNULL(prjIsFinished,0) = 0
						AND (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST((ISNULL((REPLACE((ISNULL(prjTitleGroup,'') + ISNULL(prjTitle,'') + ISNULL(prjPostFix,'')),' ' ,'')),'')) AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
						= 
						(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST((ISNULL((REPLACE((ISNULL(@prjTitleGroup,'') + ISNULL(@prjTitle,'') + ISNULL(@prjPostFix,'')),' ' ,'')),'')) AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
					)
IF(ISNULL(@PrdOrdIssuePrjUsedType,0) = 1)/*(Issue in an existing project)*/
BEGIN
/**/
	IF(ISNULL(@ProjectId,0) = 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @ProjectId = -1
	/**/
	END
/**/
END
ELSE IF(ISNULL(@PrdOrdIssuePrjUsedType,0) = 2)/*(Issuing in a new project)*/
BEGIN
/**/
	SET @ProjectId = -1
/**/
END
ELSE IF(ISNULL(@PrdOrdIssuePrjUsedType,0) = 3)/*(No issuance in the absence of a project)*/
BEGIN
/**/
	IF(ISNULL(@ProjectId,0) = 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @ProjectId = -2
	/**/
	END
/**/
END
/*----------------------------*/
IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
/**/
	SELECT 
		 @ProjectId [ProjectId]
		,[ProductID]
		,(SELECT TOP (1) [ProjectTitleGroup] FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectTitleGroups] PTG WHERE PTG.[ProjectTitleGroupId] = [PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleGroupId]) [PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleGroup]
		,(SELECT TOP (1) [ProjectTitle] FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectTitles] PT WHERE PT.[ProjectTitleID] = [PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleId]) [PrdOrdIssuePrjTitle]
		,[PrdOrdIssuePrjPostFix]		
	FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] P
	WHERE P.ProductID = @ProductId	
/**/
END
ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
/**/
	SELECT 
		 @ProjectId [ProjectId]
		,[ProductID]
		,(SELECT TOP (1) [ProjectTitleGroup] FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectTitleGroups_Lang] PTG WHERE PTG.[ProjectTitleGroupId] = [PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleGroupId] AND PTG.LanguageID = @LanguageID) [PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleGroup]
		,(SELECT TOP (1) [ProjectTitle] FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectTitles_Lang] PT WHERE PT.[ProjectTitleID] = [PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleId] AND PT.LanguageID = @LanguageID) [PrdOrdIssuePrjTitle]
		,(SELECT TOP (1) [PrdOrdIssuePrjPostFix] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts_Lang] PLN WHERE PLN.[ProductID] = P.[ProductID] AND PLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID) [PrdOrdIssuePrjPostFix]		
	FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] P
	WHERE P.ProductID = @ProductId	
/**/
END
/*----------------------------------------------------*/
/**/
END







GO
-- [dbo].[MobspCloseTicket]





/*AI14010317 - Copied from web*/
/*AI14010317 - remove condition*/
/*AI14010415 - Add prjFinishedDuration*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspCloseTicket]
(	
/* 
	EXEC MobspCloseTicket 62,1
*/
@ProjectId INT,
@UserId INT,
@Year1 INT,
@Month1 INT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@AppType VARCHAR(20)
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/	
/*----------------------- define variabels ---------------------*/
DECLARE @RegisterTime NVARCHAR(8),@RegisterDay NVARCHAR(10)
SET @RegisterTime = CAST(@RegisterDate AS TIME)
SET @RegisterDay = DATEPART(dw,@RegisterDate)
/*----------------------------------------------*/
	UPDATE [dbo].[CrmProjects]
	SET  [prjIsFinished] = 1
		,[prjFinishedDate] = @RegisterDatePersian
		,[prjFinishedDay] = @RegisterDay
		,[prjFinishedTime] = @RegisterTime
		,[prjFinishedDateTime] = @RegisterDate
		,[prjFinishedUserId] = @UserId
		,[UserId] = @UserId
		,[Year1] = @Year1
		,[Month1] = @Month1
		,[RegisterDate] = @RegisterDate
		,[RegisterDatePersian] = @RegisterDatePersian		
		,[AppType] = @AppType				
		,[prjToDoTimeDone] = @RegisterDate
		,[prjFinishedDuration] = DATEDIFF(SECOND, prjStartDateTime, @RegisterDate)/*AI14010415*/
	WHERE
	/*ISNULL([prjFOCmpCanSee], 0)  = 0 AI14010317*/
	ISNULL([prjIsTicket], 0)  = 1
	AND ProjectID = @ProjectId
/**/
END






GO

		WHERE PreOrderPaymentId IN(SELECT [PreOrderPaymentId]		    
									FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderPayments]
									WHERE [pordpayPreOrderId] IN(SELECT [PreOrderID]      
																 FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder]
																 WHERE [pordCompanyId] = @CompanyId
																 AND [pordProjectId] = @ProjectId))

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderPayments]
		WHERE [pordpayPreOrderId] IN(SELECT [PreOrderID]      
									 FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder]
									 WHERE [pordCompanyId] = @CompanyId
									 AND [pordProjectId] = @ProjectId)
		/**/
		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItemSpecification_Lang]
		WHERE [PreOrderItemSpcflID] IN(SELECT [PreOrderItemSpcflID]		
										FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItemSpecification]
										WHERE [PreOrderItemId] IN (SELECT [PreOrderItemID]
																	FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems]
																	WHERE [PreOrderId] IN(SELECT [PreOrderID]      
																						  FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder]
																						  WHERE [pordCompanyId] = @CompanyId
																						  AND [pordProjectId] = @ProjectId)))
		
		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItemSpecification]
		WHERE [PreOrderItemId] IN (SELECT [PreOrderItemID]
								 FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems]
								 WHERE [PreOrderId] IN(SELECT [PreOrderID]      
														FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder]
														WHERE [pordCompanyId] = @CompanyId
														AND [pordProjectId] = @ProjectId))
		/**/
		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems_Lang]
		WHERE PreOrderItemID IN (SELECT [PreOrderItemID]
								 FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems]
								 WHERE [PreOrderId] IN(SELECT [PreOrderID]      
														FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder]
														WHERE [pordCompanyId] = @CompanyId
														AND [pordProjectId] = @ProjectId))

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems]
		WHERE [PreOrderId] IN(SELECT [PreOrderID]      
							  FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder]
							  WHERE [pordCompanyId] = @CompanyId
							  AND [pordProjectId] = @ProjectId)
		/**/
		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder_Lang]
		WHERE [PreOrderID] IN(SELECT [PreOrderID]      
							  FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder]
							  WHERE [pordCompanyId] = @CompanyId
							  AND [pordProjectId] = @ProjectId)

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder]
		WHERE [pordCompanyId] = @CompanyId
		AND [pordProjectId] = @ProjectId

		/*------------ Conversation ------------*/
		DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmConversation_Lang]
		WHERE ConversationID IN(SELECT [ConversationID]     
								FROM [dbo].[CrmConversation]
								WHERE [cnvsCompanyId] = @CompanyId
								AND [cnvsProjectId] = @ProjectId)

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmConversation]
		WHERE [cnvsCompanyId] = @CompanyId
		AND [cnvsProjectId] = @ProjectId	
		

		/*------------ CrmProjectProductHistory ------------*/
		/*AI14020420- Add delete prjPrdHistory*/
		DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectProductHistory_Lang]
		WHERE [ProjectProductHistoryId] IN(SELECT [ProjectProductHistoryId]     
								FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectProductHistory]
								WHERE [PrjPrdHCompanyId] = @CompanyId
								AND [PrjPrdHProjectId] = @ProjectId)

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectProductHistory]
		WHERE [PrjPrdHCompanyId] = @CompanyId
		AND [PrjPrdHProjectId] = @ProjectId	
		/*AI14020420- End*/
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		SET @Result = '1'
    COMMIT
/**/
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
/**/
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK
	SET @Result = '0'
/**/
END CATCH
/**/
END

SELECT @Result AS [Result]

/**/
END

/**/
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK
	SET @Result = '0'

END

		/*AI14020420- Add delete prjPrdHistory*/

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectProductHistory]
		WHERE [PrjPrdHCompanyId] = @CompanyId		
		/*AI14020420- End*/		
		/*------------ Projects ------------*/
		DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects_Lang]
		WHERE ProjectID IN(SELECT [ProjectID]           
							FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects]
							WHERE [prjCompanyId] = @CompanyId)

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects]
		WHERE [prjCompanyId] = @CompanyId		
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		SET @Result = '1'
    COMMIT
/**/
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
/**/
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK
	SET @Result = '0'
/**/
END CATCH
/**/
END

SELECT @Result AS [Result]

/**/
END







GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspDeleteAllCompanyWithDependencies]
(		
/*
	EXEC MobspDeleteAllCompanyWithDependencies 6131
*/
@CompanyId INT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*------------------------- Find Order,BuyOrder,Doc,Ctsh ------------------*/
DECLARE @CanDelete BIT = 1,@Result NVARCHAR(50) = N''

IF(EXISTS(SELECT * FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] WHERE [ordCompanyId]= @CompanyId))/*Ord*/
BEGIN
	SET @CanDelete = 0
	IF(ISNULL(@Result,'') = '')
		SET @Result = '-1'
	ELSE 
		SET @Result = ISNULL(@Result,'') + '|-1'
END
/**/
IF(EXISTS(SELECT * FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrder] WHERE [bordCompanyId]= @CompanyId))/*Bord*/
BEGIN
	SET @CanDelete = 0
	IF(ISNULL(@Result,'') = '')
		SET @Result = '-2'
	ELSE 
		SET @Result = ISNULL(@Result,'') + '|-2'
END
/**/
IF(EXISTS(SELECT * FROM [dbo].[WSDoc] WHERE [CompanyId]= @CompanyId)) /*Doc*/
BEGIN
	SET @CanDelete = 0
	IF(ISNULL(@Result,'') = '')
		SET @Result = '-3'
	ELSE 
		SET @Result = ISNULL(@Result,'') + '|-3'
END
/**/
IF(EXISTS(SELECT * FROM [dbo].[WSCostSheets] WHERE [ctshCmpProjectId] IN(SELECT [ProjectID]           
																		 FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects]
																		 WHERE [prjCompanyId] = @CompanyId))) /*Ctsh*/
BEGIN
	SET @CanDelete = 0
	IF(ISNULL(@Result,'') = '')
		SET @Result = '-4'
	ELSE 
		SET @Result = ISNULL(@Result,'') + '|-4'
END
/*--------------------------------------------------------------------*/
IF(@CanDelete = 1)
BEGIN
/**/
BEGIN TRY
/**/
    BEGIN TRANSACTION 
		/*------------------------------------------------*/
		/*------------ DyForm ------------*/
		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails_Lang]
		WHERE [VerificationRequestDetailId] IN(SELECT [VerificationRequestDetailId]      
												FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails]
												WHERE [VerificationRequestId] IN(SELECT  [VerificationRequestId]		 
																				FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
																				WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] = 80
																				AND [VerificationReqObjectId] IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
																											FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
																											WHERE [CompanyId] = @CompanyId)))

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails]
		WHERE [VerificationRequestId] IN(SELECT  [VerificationRequestId]		 
										FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]

										AND [VerificationReqObjectId] IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
																	FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]

		WHERE [CompanyId] = @CompanyId

		UPDATE [dbo].[CrmCmsComments]
		SET  [CrmCompanyId] = NULL
		WHERE [CrmCompanyId] = @CompanyId

		SET  [CrmCompanyId] = NULL			
		WHERE [CrmCompanyId] = @CompanyId

								FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects]
								WHERE [prjCompanyId] = @CompanyId)

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectInformations_Lang]
		WHERE [ProjectInformationId] IN(SELECT [ProjectInformationId]     
										FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectInformations]
										WHERE [ProjectId] IN(SELECT [ProjectID]           
															 FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects]
															 WHERE [prjCompanyId] = @CompanyId))

		WHERE [ProjectId] IN(SELECT [ProjectID]           
							 FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects]
							 WHERE [prjCompanyId] = @CompanyId)

		/*------------ ProjectMultiGroups ------------*/

		SET @Result = '1'
    COMMIT

    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0

	SET @Result = '0'

END CATCH

END

SELECT @Result AS [Result]

END

/*AI14021004*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspDeleteCompanyAddress]
(		
/*
	EXEC MobspDeleteCompanyAddress 1
*/
@CompanyAddressId INT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
BEGIN TRY
    BEGIN TRANSACTION 
			DELETE [dbo].[CrmCompanyAddresses_Lang]  WHERE [CompanyAddressId] = @CompanyAddressId
			DELETE [dbo].[CrmCompanyAddresses]  WHERE [CompanyAddressId] = @CompanyAddressId
			SELECT 1 AS Result
    COMMIT
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK	
	SELECT 0 AS Result
END CATCH
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[MobspDeleteCompanyMoneyBagFromPayment]





/*AI14020502 - Copied from web*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspDeleteCompanyMoneyBagFromPayment]
(		
/*
	EXEC MobspDeleteCompanyMoneyBagFromPayment 1,1,140
*/
@CompanyMoneyBagId INT,
@Mode INT,/*Mode = 1(Pord), Mode = 2(ord)*/
@PaymentId INT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
BEGIN TRY
    BEGIN TRANSACTION 
		/*-------------------------------------*/
		IF(@Mode = 1)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			UPDATE [dbo].[WSPreOrderPayments]
			SET pordpayCompanyMoneyBagId = NULL
			WHERE PreOrderPaymentId = @PaymentId
		/**/
		END
		ELSE IF(@Mode = 2)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			UPDATE [dbo].[WSOrderPayments]
			SET ordpayCompanyMoneyBagId = NULL
			WHERE OrderPaymentId = @PaymentId
		/**/
		END
		/*-------------------------------------*/		
		DELETE [dbo].[CrmCompanyMoneyBag_Lang]  WHERE [CompanyMoneyBagId] = @CompanyMoneyBagId
        DELETE [dbo].[CrmCompanyMoneyBag]  WHERE [CompanyMoneyBagId] = @CompanyMoneyBagId
		SELECT 1 AS Result							
    COMMIT
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK
	SELECT 0 AS Result
END CATCH
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[MobspDeleteCompanyProjectWithDependencies]






/*AI14020124 - Copied from web*/
/*AI14020420 - Add delete prjPrdHistory*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspDeleteCompanyProjectWithDependencies]
(		
/*
	EXEC MobspDeleteCompanyProjectWithDependencies 6938
*/
@ProjectId INT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*------------------------- Find Order,BuyOrder,Doc,Ctsh ------------------*/
DECLARE @CanDelete BIT = 1,@Result NVARCHAR(50) = N''
DECLARE @CompanyId INT 
SET @CompanyId = (SELECT TOP(1) [prjCompanyId] FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] WHERE ProjectID = @ProjectId)

IF(EXISTS(SELECT * FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] WHERE [ordCompanyId]= @CompanyId AND [ordProjectId] = @ProjectId))/*Ord*/
BEGIN
	SET @CanDelete = 0
	IF(ISNULL(@Result,'') = '')
		SET @Result = '-1'
	ELSE 
		SET @Result = ISNULL(@Result,'') + '|-1'
END
/**/
IF(EXISTS(SELECT * FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrder] WHERE [bordCompanyId]= @CompanyId AND [bordProjectId] = @ProjectId))/*Bord*/
BEGIN
	SET @CanDelete = 0
	IF(ISNULL(@Result,'') = '')
		SET @Result = '-2'
	ELSE 
		SET @Result = ISNULL(@Result,'') + '|-2'
END
/**/
IF(EXISTS(SELECT * FROM [dbo].[WSDoc] WHERE [CompanyId]= @CompanyId AND [ProjectId] = @ProjectId)) /*Doc*/
BEGIN
	SET @CanDelete = 0
	IF(ISNULL(@Result,'') = '')
		SET @Result = '-3'
	ELSE 
		SET @Result = ISNULL(@Result,'') + '|-3'
END
/**/
IF(EXISTS(SELECT * FROM [dbo].[WSCostSheets] WHERE [ctshCmpProjectId] = @ProjectId)) /*Ctsh*/
BEGIN
	SET @CanDelete = 0
	IF(ISNULL(@Result,'') = '')
		SET @Result = '-4'
	ELSE 
		SET @Result = ISNULL(@Result,'') + '|-4'
END
/*--------------------------------------------------------------------*/
IF(@CanDelete = 1)
BEGIN
/**/
BEGIN TRY
/**/
    BEGIN TRANSACTION 
		/*------------------------------------------------*/
		/*------------ DyForm ------------*/
		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails_Lang]
		WHERE [VerificationRequestDetailId] IN(SELECT [VerificationRequestDetailId]      
												FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails]
												WHERE [VerificationRequestId] IN(SELECT  [VerificationRequestId]		 
																				FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
																				WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] = 80
																				AND [VerificationReqObjectId] IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
																											FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
																											WHERE [ProjectId] = @ProjectId)))

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails]
		WHERE [VerificationRequestId] IN(SELECT  [VerificationRequestId]		 
										FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
										WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] = 80
										AND [VerificationReqObjectId] IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
																	FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
																	WHERE [ProjectId] = @ProjectId))

		/**/
		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests_Lang]
		WHERE [VerificationRequestId] IN(SELECT  [VerificationRequestId]		 
										FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
										WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] = 80
										AND [VerificationReqObjectId] IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
																	FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
																	WHERE [ProjectId] = @ProjectId))

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
		WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] = 80
		AND [VerificationReqObjectId] IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
									FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
									WHERE [ProjectId] = @ProjectId)

		/**/
		DELETE FROM dbo.[CrmDyFormFieldValues_Lang]  
		WHERE FieldValueId IN (SELECT FormFieldValueId 
								FROM dbo.[CrmDyFormFieldValues] 
								WHERE MasterValueId IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
														FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues] 
														WHERE [ProjectId] = @ProjectId))
		
		DELETE FROM dbo.[CrmDyFormFieldValues] 
		WHERE MasterValueId IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
								FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues] 
								WHERE [ProjectId] = @ProjectId)

		/**/
		DELETE FROM dbo.[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValueDetails] 
		WHERE MasterValueId IN(SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
								FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
								WHERE [ProjectId] = @ProjectId)
		/**/		
		DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
		WHERE [ProjectId] = @ProjectId

		/*------------ CallHistory ------------*/		
		UPDATE [dbo].[CrmCallHistory]
		SET  [ConversationID] = NULL
		WHERE [CompanyID] = @CompanyId
		AND [ConversationID] IN(SELECT CV.ConversationID 
								FROM [dbo].[CrmConversation] CV 
								WHERE CV.cnvsCompanyId = @CompanyId
								AND CV.cnvsProjectId = @ProjectId)

		/*------------ InternalMessage ------------*/	
		UPDATE [dbo].[CrmInternalMessage]
		SET  [msgProjectId] = NULL
		WHERE [msgCompanyId] = @CompanyId
		AND [msgProjectId] = @ProjectId

		/*------------ ProjectFieldTitle ------------*/
		DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectFieldTitle_Lang]
		WHERE [ProjectFieldTitleId] IN(SELECT [ProjectFieldTitleId]    
										FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectFieldTitle]
										WHERE [ProjectId] = @ProjectId)
		
		DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectFieldTitle]
		WHERE [ProjectId] = @ProjectId
		
		/*------------ ProjectInformations ------------*/
		DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectInformations_Lang]
		WHERE [ProjectInformationId] IN(SELECT [ProjectInformationId]     
										FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectInformations]
										WHERE [ProjectId] = @ProjectId)

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectInformations]
		WHERE [ProjectId] = @ProjectId

		/*------------ ProjectMultiGroups ------------*/
		DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectMultiGroups]
		WHERE [ProjectID] = @ProjectId 

		/*------------ ProjectMultiGroups ------------*/
		DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectMultiIGroups]
		WHERE [ProjectID] = @ProjectId
							 
		/*------------ Emails ------------*/		
		DELETE FROM [dbo].[EmailUserTempTank]
		WHERE [EmailCmpId] = @CompanyId
		AND [EmailCmpPrjId] = @ProjectId
		/**/
		UPDATE [dbo].[EmailReceive]
		SET  [ProjectId] = NULL			
		WHERE [CompanyId] = @CompanyId
		AND [ProjectId] = @ProjectId
		/**/
		UPDATE [dbo].[EmailReceiveAttachments]
		SET  [ProjectId] = NULL			
		WHERE [CompanyId] = @CompanyId
		AND [ProjectId] = @ProjectId
		/**/		
		UPDATE [dbo].[EmailSendDetail]
		SET  [EmailCmpPrjId] = NULL				
		WHERE [EmailCmpId] = @CompanyId			
		AND [EmailCmpPrjId] = @ProjectId
		/*------------ SecLetters ------------*/
		DELETE FROM [dbo].[SecLetters_Lang]
		WHERE [LetterId] IN(SELECT [LetterId]      
							FROM [dbo].[SecLetters]
							WHERE [LetterCompanyId] = @CompanyId
							AND [LettercmpProjectId] = @ProjectId)

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[SecLetters]
		WHERE [LetterCompanyId] = @CompanyId
		AND [LettercmpProjectId] = @ProjectId

		/*------------ SMS ------------*/				
		UPDATE [dbo].[SmsSendDetail]
		SET  [CompanyProjectId] = NULL			
		WHERE [CompanyId] = @CompanyId
		AND CompanyProjectId = @ProjectId
		
		/*------------ FilesDirectory ------------*/	
		DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmFilesDirectory_Lang]
		WHERE [FileDirectoryID] IN(SELECT [FileDirectoryID]     
									FROM [dbo].[CrmFilesDirectory]
									WHERE [MasterId] = @CompanyId
									AND [SubDetailId] = @ProjectId)

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmFilesDirectory]
		WHERE [MasterId] = @CompanyId
		AND [SubDetailId] = @ProjectId

		/*------------ PreOrder ------------*/	

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails_Lang]
		WHERE [VerificationRequestDetailId] IN(SELECT [VerificationRequestDetailId]      
												FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails]
												WHERE [VerificationRequestId] IN(SELECT  [VerificationRequestId]		 
																				FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
																				WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] = 10
																				AND [VerificationReqObjectId] IN(SELECT [PreOrderID]      
																												FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder]
																												WHERE [pordCompanyId] = @CompanyId
																												AND [pordProjectId] = @ProjectId)))

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails]
		WHERE [VerificationRequestId] IN(SELECT  [VerificationRequestId]		 
										FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
										WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] = 10
										AND [VerificationReqObjectId] IN(SELECT [PreOrderID]      
																		FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder]
																		WHERE [pordCompanyId] = @CompanyId
																		AND [pordProjectId] = @ProjectId))

		/**/
		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests_Lang]
		WHERE [VerificationRequestId] IN(SELECT  [VerificationRequestId]		 
										FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
										WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] = 10
										AND [VerificationReqObjectId] IN(SELECT [PreOrderID]      
																		FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder]
																		WHERE [pordCompanyId] = @CompanyId
																		AND [pordProjectId] = @ProjectId))

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
		WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] = 10
		AND [VerificationReqObjectId] IN(SELECT [PreOrderID]      
										FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder]
										WHERE [pordCompanyId] = @CompanyId
										AND [pordProjectId] = @ProjectId)

		/**/
		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderPrdGroupMustExistReasons_Lang]
		WHERE [PrdGroupMustExistReasonId] IN(SELECT [PrdGroupMustExistReasonId]		 
											 FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderPrdGroupMustExistReasons]
											 WHERE [PreOrderId] IN(SELECT [PreOrderID]      
																	FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder]
																	WHERE [pordCompanyId] = @CompanyId
																	AND [pordProjectId] = @ProjectId))

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderPrdGroupMustExistReasons]
		WHERE [PreOrderId] IN(SELECT [PreOrderID]      
							  FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder]
							  WHERE [pordCompanyId] = @CompanyId
							  AND [pordProjectId] = @ProjectId)
		/**/
		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderCommissions_Lang]
		WHERE [PreOrderCommissionId] IN(SELECT [PreOrderCommissionId]      
										FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderCommissions]
										WHERE [CommPreOrderId] IN(SELECT [PreOrderID]      
																FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder]
																WHERE [pordCompanyId] = @CompanyId
																AND [pordProjectId] = @ProjectId))

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderCommissions]
		WHERE [CommPreOrderId] IN(SELECT [PreOrderID]      
								FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder]
								WHERE [pordCompanyId] = @CompanyId
								AND [pordProjectId] = @ProjectId)
		/**/
		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderPayments_Lang]
		WHERE PreOrderPaymentId IN(SELECT [PreOrderPaymentId]		    
									FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderPayments]
									WHERE [pordpayPreOrderId] IN(SELECT [PreOrderID]      
																 FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder]
																 WHERE [pordCompanyId] = @CompanyId
																 AND [pordProjectId] = @ProjectId))

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderPayments]
		WHERE [pordpayPreOrderId] IN(SELECT [PreOrderID]      
									 FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder]
									 WHERE [pordCompanyId] = @CompanyId
									 AND [pordProjectId] = @ProjectId)
		/**/
		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItemSpecification_Lang]
		WHERE [PreOrderItemSpcflID] IN(SELECT [PreOrderItemSpcflID]		
										FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItemSpecification]
										WHERE [PreOrderItemId] IN (SELECT [PreOrderItemID]
																	FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems]
																	WHERE [PreOrderId] IN(SELECT [PreOrderID]      
																						  FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder]
																						  WHERE [pordCompanyId] = @CompanyId
																						  AND [pordProjectId] = @ProjectId)))
		
		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItemSpecification]
		WHERE [PreOrderItemId] IN (SELECT [PreOrderItemID]
								 FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems]
								 WHERE [PreOrderId] IN(SELECT [PreOrderID]      
														FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder]
														WHERE [pordCompanyId] = @CompanyId
														AND [pordProjectId] = @ProjectId))
		/**/
		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems_Lang]
		WHERE PreOrderItemID IN (SELECT [PreOrderItemID]
								 FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems]
								 WHERE [PreOrderId] IN(SELECT [PreOrderID]      
														FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder]
														WHERE [pordCompanyId] = @CompanyId
														AND [pordProjectId] = @ProjectId))

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems]
		WHERE [PreOrderId] IN(SELECT [PreOrderID]      
							  FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder]
							  WHERE [pordCompanyId] = @CompanyId
							  AND [pordProjectId] = @ProjectId)
		/**/
		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder_Lang]
		WHERE [PreOrderID] IN(SELECT [PreOrderID]      
							  FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder]
							  WHERE [pordCompanyId] = @CompanyId
							  AND [pordProjectId] = @ProjectId)

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder]
		WHERE [pordCompanyId] = @CompanyId
		AND [pordProjectId] = @ProjectId
		
		/*------------ Conversation ------------*/
		DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmConversation_Lang]
		WHERE ConversationID IN(SELECT [ConversationID]     
								FROM [dbo].[CrmConversation]
								WHERE [cnvsCompanyId] = @CompanyId
								AND [cnvsProjectId] = @ProjectId)

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmConversation]
		WHERE [cnvsCompanyId] = @CompanyId
		AND [cnvsProjectId] = @ProjectId
		/*------------ CrmProjectProductHistory ------------*/
		/*AI14020420- Add delete prjPrdHistory*/
		DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectProductHistory_Lang]
		WHERE [ProjectProductHistoryId] IN(SELECT [ProjectProductHistoryId]     
								FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectProductHistory]
								WHERE [PrjPrdHCompanyId] = @CompanyId
								AND [PrjPrdHProjectId] = @ProjectId)

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectProductHistory]
		WHERE [PrjPrdHCompanyId] = @CompanyId
		AND [PrjPrdHProjectId] = @ProjectId	
		/*AI14020420- End*/
		/*------------ Projects ------------*/
		DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects_Lang]
		WHERE ProjectID = @ProjectId

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects]
		WHERE [prjCompanyId] = @CompanyId	
		AND [ProjectID] = @ProjectId
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		SET @Result = '1'
    COMMIT
/**/
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
/**/
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK
	SET @Result = '0'
/**/
END CATCH
/**/
END

SELECT @Result AS [Result]

/**/
END

-- [dbo].[MobspDeleteCrmCompany]



/*RZ961207*/
/*RZ970309- Delete dependency*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[MobspDeleteCrmCompany]
(		
/*
	exec MobspDeleteCrmCompany 6453
*/
@CompanyId INT
)
As
BEGIN
/**/
BEGIN TRY
    BEGIN TRANSACTION 
		/*RZ970309- Delete dependency*/
		EXEC dbo.CRMspDeleteCompanyBackDependencies @CompanyId
		/*!RZ970309- Delete dependency*/
		DELETE dbo.CrmCompany_Lang  WHERE CompanyID = @CompanyId
        DELETE dbo.CrmCompany  WHERE CompanyID = @CompanyId
        /*Check for delete*/
        DECLARE @FindRecordId INT
        SET @FindRecordId = (SELECT TOP 1 CompanyID FROM dbo.CrmCompany  WHERE CompanyID = @CompanyId)
        IF(@FindRecordId IS NULL) /*delete*/
			SELECT 1 as Result 
		ELSE
			SELECT -1 as Result /*not delete*/
    COMMIT
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK
	SELECT 0 as Result
END CATCH
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[MobspDeleteCrmConversation]






/*RZ961207*/
/*AI14010415 - Set null in prjResponseDate and prjFirstDoingDate in ticket*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspDeleteCrmConversation]
(		
/*
	EXEC MobspDeleteCrmConversation 25566
*/
@ConversationId INT
)
As
BEGIN
/**/
BEGIN TRY
    BEGIN TRANSACTION 

		/*RZ14010414- Set null in prjResponseDate and prjFirstDoingDate in ticket*/
		DECLARE @cnvsIsTicket BIT,@cnvsProjectId INT,@CnvsCnt INT
		SELECT TOP(1) 
			@cnvsIsTicket = cnvsIsTicket ,
			@cnvsProjectId = cnvsProjectId
		FROM CrmConversation 
		WHERE ConversationID = @ConversationID
		/**/		
		IF(ISNULL(@cnvsIsTicket,0) = 1) 
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @CnvsCnt = (SELECT COUNT(*)
							FROM CrmConversation
							WHERE cnvsProjectId = @cnvsProjectId)
			IF(ISNULL(@CnvsCnt,0) = 1)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				UPDATE dbo.CrmProjects
				SET [prjFirstDoingDate] = NULL,
					[prjFirstDoingDuration] = NULL,
					[prjFirstDoingUserId] = NULL,
					[prjResponseDate] = NULL,
					[prjResponseDuration] = NULL,
					[prjResponseUserId] = NULL,
					[prjReadDate] = NULL,
					[prjReadUserId] = NULL,
					[prjReadDuration] = NULL
				WHERE ProjectID = @cnvsProjectId
			/**/
			END
			ELSE IF(ISNULL(@CnvsCnt,0) > 1)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @CnvsCnt = (SELECT COUNT(*)
								FROM CrmConversation
								WHERE cnvsProjectId = @cnvsProjectId
								AND cnvsStatusProcessKindId IN(1,3)
								AND ISNULL(cnvsIsCopy,0) = 0)

				/*-------------------------------*/
				DECLARE @cnvsStatusProcessKindId INT
				SET @cnvsStatusProcessKindId = (SELECT TOP(1) cnvsStatusProcessKindId 
							   FROM CrmConversation
							   WHERE  ConversationID = @ConversationID
							   AND ISNULL(cnvsIsCopy,0) = 0)

				/*-------------------------------*/
				IF(ISNULL(@CnvsCnt,0) = 1)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					IF(ISNULL(@cnvsStatusProcessKindId,0) = 1 OR ISNULL(@cnvsStatusProcessKindId,0) = 3) /*Doing Or Done*/
					BEGIN
					/**/
						UPDATE dbo.CrmProjects
						SET [prjFirstDoingDate] = NULL,
							[prjFirstDoingDuration] = NULL,
							[prjFirstDoingUserId] = NULL
						WHERE ProjectID = @cnvsProjectId
					/**/
					END										
				/**/
				END	
				/*-------------------------------*/
				SET @CnvsCnt = NULL
				SET @CnvsCnt = (SELECT COUNT(*)
								FROM CrmConversation
								WHERE cnvsProjectId = @cnvsProjectId
								AND FirstRegisterUserId > 0
								AND ISNULL(cnvsIsCopy,0) = 0)
				/*-------------------------------*/
				DECLARE @cnvsId INT
				SET @cnvsId = (SELECT TOP(1) ConversationID 
							   FROM CrmConversation
							   WHERE cnvsProjectId = @cnvsProjectId
							   AND FirstRegisterUserId > 0
							   AND ISNULL(cnvsIsCopy,0) = 0)

				IF(ISNULL(@CnvsCnt,0) = 1)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					IF(ISNULL(@cnvsId,0) = @ConversationID)
					BEGIN
					/**/
						UPDATE dbo.CrmProjects
						SET [prjResponseDate] = NULL,
							[prjResponseDuration] = NULL,
							[prjResponseUserId] = NULL
						WHERE ProjectID = @cnvsProjectId
					/**/
					END
				/**/
				END				
				/*-------------------------------*/
			/**/
			END
		/**/
		END
		/*RZ14010414- End*/

		DELETE dbo.CrmConversation_Lang  WHERE ConversationID = @ConversationId
        DELETE dbo.CrmConversation  WHERE ConversationID = @ConversationId
        /*Check for delete*/
        DECLARE @FindRecordId INT
        SET @FindRecordId = (SELECT TOP 1 ConversationID FROM dbo.CrmConversation  WHERE ConversationID = @ConversationId)
        IF(@FindRecordId IS NULL) /*delete*/
			SELECT 1 as Result 
		ELSE
			SELECT -1 as Result /*not delete*/                
    COMMIT
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK
    SELECT 0 as Result 
END CATCH
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[MobspDeleteCrmProject]



/*RZ961207*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[MobspDeleteCrmProject]
(		
/*
	exec MobspDeleteCrmProject 7060, 6298, 5
*/
@ProjectId INT,
@CompanyId INT, 
@prjcnvsStatusGroupId INT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
BEGIN TRY
    BEGIN TRANSACTION 
		/*Check allow delete*/
		DECLARE @CrmProjectMultiGroup INT, @CrmProjectMultiIGroup INT, @CountConversation INT = 0 
		SET @CrmProjectMultiGroup = (SELECT TOP 1 [ProjectID] FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectMultiGroups] WHERE [ProjectID] = @ProjectId)
		/**/
		SET @CrmProjectMultiIGroup = (SELECT TOP 1 [ProjectID] FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectMultiIGroups] WHERE [ProjectID] = @ProjectId)
		/**/
		EXEC MobspCheckUsedPrjCNVSStatusGroup  
		@CompanyId = @CompanyId,
		@ProjectId = @ProjectId,
		@prjcnvsStatusGroupId = @prjcnvsStatusGroupId,
		@CountConversation = @CountConversation OUTPUT
		IF(@CrmProjectMultiGroup IS NULL AND @CrmProjectMultiIGroup IS NULL AND @CountConversation = 0) /*delete*/
		BEGIN
			/*project*/
			DELETE dbo.CrmProjects_Lang  WHERE [ProjectID] = @ProjectId
			DELETE dbo.CrmProjects  WHERE [ProjectID] = @ProjectId
			/*Check for delete*/
			DECLARE @FindRecordId INT
			SET @FindRecordId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ProjectID] FROM dbo.CrmProjects  WHERE [ProjectID] = @ProjectId)
			IF(@FindRecordId IS NULL) /*delete*/
				SELECT 1 as Result 
			ELSE
				SELECT -1 as Result /*not delete*/
			END
		ELSE
		BEGIN /*Redundancy*/
			SELECT 0 as Result /*Redundancy*/
		END    		
    COMMIT
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK
    SELECT 0 as Result
END CATCH
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[MobspDeleteDocItemsAll]






/*AI14020530 - Copied from web*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspDeleteDocItemsAll]
(		
/*
	EXEC MobspDeleteDocItemsAll 559
*/
@DocId INT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
BEGIN TRY
    BEGIN TRANSACTION 
		/*---------------------------------------------------------------*/						
		DELETE [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials_Lang] 
		WHERE DocItemSerialID IN (SELECT 
										DocItemSerialID 
									FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials]
									WHERE ItemId IN(SELECT DocItemID
													FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems]  
													WHERE DocID = @DocId))

		DELETE [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials] WHERE ItemId IN(SELECT DocItemID
														FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems]  
														WHERE DocID = @DocId)	
		
		/*-----------------------*/

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems_Lang]  
		WHERE DocItemID IN(SELECT DocItemID
							FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems]  
							WHERE DocID = @DocId)

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems]  WHERE DocID = @DocId

		/*---------------------------------------------------------------*/
		SELECT 1 AS Result
    COMMIT
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK
	SELECT 0 AS Result
END CATCH
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[MobspDeleteDocItemWithLinkedPrd]






/*AI14020601 - Copied from web*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspDeleteDocItemWithLinkedPrd]
(		
/*
	EXEC MobspDeleteDocItemWithLinkedPrd 559,2200
*/
@DocId INT,
@DocItemId INT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
BEGIN TRY
    BEGIN TRANSACTION 
		/*---------------------------------------------------------------*/
		/*------------------------ Delete items by linked -----------------*/
		DECLARE @ProductId INT
		SET @ProductId = (SELECT TOP 1 [PrdId] FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems] WHERE [DocItemID] = @DocItemId) 
				
		DELETE [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials_Lang] 
		WHERE DocItemSerialID IN (SELECT 
											DocItemSerialID 
									   FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials]
									   WHERE ItemId IN (SELECT 
																	I.DocItemID 
																 FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems] I 
																 WHERE DocId = @DocId
																 AND [PrdId] IN(SELECT 
																						(CASE WHEN PrdId = @ProductId 
																							THEN LinkedPrdId
																							ELSE PrdId									
																						END) [LinkedPrdId]			 
																					 FROM [dbo].[WSLinkedProducts]	
																					 WHERE ([PrdId] = @ProductId	
																					 OR [LinkedPrdId] = @ProductId)			
																					 )))

		DELETE [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials] 
		WHERE ItemId IN (SELECT 
									I.DocItemID 
								  FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems] I 
								  WHERE DocId = @DocId
								  AND [PrdId] IN(SELECT 
														(CASE WHEN PrdId = @ProductId 
															THEN LinkedPrdId
															ELSE PrdId									
														END) [LinkedPrdId]			 
													 FROM [dbo].[WSLinkedProducts]	
													 WHERE ([PrdId] = @ProductId	
													 OR [LinkedPrdId] = @ProductId)			
													 ))

		/*-----------------------*/

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems_Lang]  
		WHERE DocItemID IN (SELECT I.DocItemID FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems] I 
								WHERE DocId = @DocId
								AND [PrdId] IN(SELECT 
															(CASE WHEN PrdId = @ProductId 
																THEN LinkedPrdId
																ELSE PrdId									
															END) [LinkedPrdId]			 
													FROM [dbo].[WSLinkedProducts]	
													WHERE ([PrdId] = @ProductId	
													OR 	[LinkedPrdId] = @ProductId)			
													))

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems] 
		WHERE DocId = @DocId
		AND [PrdId] IN (SELECT 
								 (CASE WHEN PrdId = @ProductId 
									   THEN LinkedPrdId
									   ELSE PrdId									
								 END) [LinkedPrdId]			 
							FROM [dbo].[WSLinkedProducts]	
							WHERE ([PrdId] = @ProductId	
							OR 	[LinkedPrdId] = @ProductId)			
							)
		
		/*---------------------------------------------------------------*/
		/*---------------------- Delete main item -----------------------*/
		
		DELETE [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials_Lang] 
		WHERE DocItemSerialID IN (SELECT 
										DocItemSerialID 
									FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials]
									WHERE ItemId = @DocItemId)
		DELETE [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials] WHERE ItemId = @DocItemId		
		
		/*-----------------------*/

		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems_Lang]  WHERE DocItemID = @DocItemId
		DELETE FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems]  WHERE DocItemID = @DocItemId

		/*---------------------------------------------------------------*/
		SELECT 1 AS Result
    COMMIT
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK
	SELECT 0 AS Result
END CATCH
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[MobspDeletePreOrderItemSpecification]






/*AI981222*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspDeletePreOrderItemSpecification]
(	
/* 
	exec MobspDeletePreOrderItemSpecification 7875  
*/			
@PreOrderItemId INT 
)
As
BEGIN
/**/
BEGIN TRY
    BEGIN TRANSACTION 
			DELETE dbo.WSPreOrderItemSpecification_Lang

    COMMIT
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK
	Select 0 as Result
END CATCH

END

        SET @FindRecordId = (SELECT TOP 1 [OrderID] FROM [dbo].[WSOrder]  WHERE [OrderID] = @OrderId)

			SELECT 1 as Result
