-- [dbo].[MobspDeleteWSOrderItems]






/*AI14000804*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspDeleteWSOrderItems]
(		
/*
	EXEC MobspDeleteWSOrderItems 6453
*/
@OrderItemId INT
)
As
BEGIN
/**/
BEGIN TRY
    BEGIN TRANSACTION
		/*Check if item exists*/
		DECLARE @FindRecordId INT
        SET @FindRecordId = (SELECT TOP 1 [OrderItemID] FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] WHERE [OrderItemID] = @OrderItemId)
		IF(@FindRecordId IS NULL)
			SELECT -2 AS Result /*Not exist*/
		ELSE IF(@FindRecordId > 0)
		BEGIN
			DECLARE @OrdId INT, @Month1 INT, @DefLanguageId INT
			SET @OrdId = (SELECT TOP 1 [OrderId] FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems]  WHERE [OrderItemID] = @OrderItemId)
			SET @Month1 = CAST(SUBSTRING(((SELECT dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithOutSlash](CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE)))),5,2) AS INT)
			SET @DefLanguageId = (SELECT TOP 1 DefaultLanguageID FROM dbo.CrmsysSetting)
			/**/
			DELETE [dbo].[WSOrderItems_Lang] WHERE [OrderItemID] = @OrderItemId
			DELETE [dbo].[WSOrderItems]  WHERE [OrderItemID] = @OrderItemId

			/*Update order amount*/
			EXEC [dbo].[MobspCalcAndUpdateOrderAmounts] @OrdId, @Month1, @DefLanguageId, @DefLanguageId

			/*Check if is deleted*/
			SET @FindRecordId = (SELECT TOP 1 [OrderItemID] FROM WSOrderItems  WHERE [OrderItemID] = @OrderItemId)
			IF(@FindRecordId IS NULL)
				SELECT 1 AS Result  /*deleted*/
			ELSE
				SELECT -1 AS Result /*not deleted*/
		END
    COMMIT
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK
	SELECT 0 AS Result
END CATCH
/**/
END







GO
-- [dbo].[MobspDeleteWSOrderPayments]







/*AI14000804*/
/*AI14020502 - Delete money bag*/
/*AI14020830 - UpdateOrderLinkAccConflictByPay*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspDeleteWSOrderPayments]
(		
/*
	EXEC MobspDeleteWSOrderPayments 6453
*/
@OrderPaymentId INT
)
As
BEGIN
/**/
BEGIN TRY
    BEGIN TRANSACTION
		/*Check if item exists*/
		DECLARE @FindRecordId INT
        SET @FindRecordId = (SELECT TOP 1 [OrderPaymentId] FROM [dbo].[WSOrderPayments]  WHERE [OrderPaymentId] = @OrderPaymentId)
		IF(@FindRecordId IS NULL)
			SELECT -2 AS Result /*Not exist*/
		ELSE IF(@FindRecordId > 0)
		BEGIN
			/*AI14020502*/
			DECLARE @ordpayCompanyMoneyBagId INT = (
						SELECT [ordpayCompanyMoneyBagId] 
						FROM [dbo].[WSOrderPayments]
						WHERE [OrderPaymentId] = @OrderPaymentId)

			DECLARE @OrdId INT, @Month1 INT, @DefLanguageId INT
			SET @OrdId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ordpayOrderId] FROM [dbo].[WSOrderPayments]  WHERE [OrderPaymentId] = @OrderPaymentId)
			SET @Month1 = CAST(SUBSTRING(((SELECT dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithOutSlash](CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE)))),5,2) AS INT)
			SET @DefLanguageId = (SELECT TOP 1 DefaultLanguageID FROM dbo.CrmsysSetting)
			/**/
			DELETE [dbo].[WSOrderPayments_Lang] WHERE [OrderPaymentId] = @OrderPaymentId
			DELETE [dbo].[WSOrderPayments]  WHERE [OrderPaymentId] = @OrderPaymentId

			/*Update order amount*/
			EXEC [dbo].[MobspCalcAndUpdateOrderAmounts] @OrdId, @Month1, @DefLanguageId, @DefLanguageId

			EXEC [dbo].[MobspUpdateOrderLinkAccConflictByPay] @OrdId /*AI14020830*/

			/*AI14020502*/
			IF(ISNULL(@ordpayCompanyMoneyBagId, 0) <> 0)
			BEGIN
				DELETE [dbo].[CrmCompanyMoneyBag_Lang]  WHERE [CompanyMoneyBagId] = @ordpayCompanyMoneyBagId
				DELETE [dbo].[CrmCompanyMoneyBag]  WHERE [CompanyMoneyBagId] = @ordpayCompanyMoneyBagId
			END

			/*Check if is deleted*/
			SET @FindRecordId = (SELECT TOP 1 [OrderPaymentId] FROM [dbo].[WSOrderPayments]  WHERE [OrderPaymentId] = @OrderPaymentId)
			IF(@FindRecordId IS NULL)
				SELECT 1 AS Result  /*deleted*/
			ELSE
				SELECT -1 AS Result /*not deleted*/
		END
    COMMIT
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK
	SELECT 0 AS Result
END CATCH
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[MobspDyDeleteFormFieldMasterValuesBySysPart]





/*AI14010523 - Copied from web*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspDyDeleteFormFieldMasterValuesBySysPart]
(		
/*
	EXEC MobspDyDeleteFormFieldMasterValuesBySysPart 6875,N'Cmp'
*/
@ObjectId INT,
@SysPart NVARCHAR(50)
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
	DECLARE @Qry NVARCHAR(MAX)='' , @Trem NVARCHAR(MAX)
	IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'cmp')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [CompanyId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'prs')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [CompanyPersonId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'prj')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [ProjectId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'cnvs')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [ConversationId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'pord')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [MasterObjectId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'ord')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [MasterObjectId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'bord')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [MasterObjectId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'ssec')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [MasterObjectId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'rsec')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [MasterObjectId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'whdoc')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [MasterObjectId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'whdraft')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [MasterObjectId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'whmovement')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [MasterObjectId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'prd')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [MasterObjectId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'cms')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [MasterObjectId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))		
	/**/
	END
	/**/
	SET @Qry = @Qry + N'DELETE dbo.[CrmDyFormFieldValues_Lang]  WHERE FieldValueId IN 
			(SELECT FormFieldValueId 
			 FROM dbo.[CrmDyFormFieldValues] 
			 WHERE MasterValueId IN(
									SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
									FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues] 
									WHERE ' + @Trem +
									' AND [FormId] IN (
											SELECT [FormId]
											FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
											WHERE [CrmSysPartId] IN (SELECT [SystemPartId] FROM [dbo].[CrmSystemParts] WHERE LOWER([Part]) = LOWER('''+@SysPart+'''))
											OR [FOSysPartId] IN (SELECT [SystemPartId] FROM [dbo].[CrmSystemParts] WHERE LOWER([Part]) = LOWER('''+@SysPart+'''))  
											)
									) ) /**/ '

	SET @Qry = @Qry + N'DELETE dbo.[CrmDyFormFieldValues] 
						WHERE MasterValueId IN(
								SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
								FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
								WHERE ' + @Trem +
								' AND [FormId] IN (
										SELECT [FormId]
										FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
										WHERE [CrmSysPartId] IN (SELECT [SystemPartId] FROM [dbo].[CrmSystemParts] WHERE LOWER([Part]) = LOWER('''+@SysPart+'''))
										OR [FOSysPartId] IN (SELECT [SystemPartId] FROM [dbo].[CrmSystemParts] WHERE LOWER([Part]) = LOWER('''+@SysPart+'''))  
										)
							) /**/ '

	SET @Qry = @Qry + N'DELETE dbo.[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValueDetails] 
						WHERE MasterValueId IN(
								SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
								FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
								WHERE ' + @Trem +

										SELECT [FormId]
										FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]

	SET @Qry = @Qry + N'DELETE [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
						WHERE ' + @Trem +

						SELECT [FormId]
						FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]

-- [dbo].[MobspDyFormCheck]







/*AI14010512 - Copied from web*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspDyFormCheck]
(
/* 
	EXEC MobspDyFormCheck 357, 3,'13970824' ,2
*/
@MasterValueId INT,
@CheckerUserId INT,
@CheckedDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@Mode INT /*mode=1 ===> form is normal, mode=2 ===> form is a workflow*/
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
	IF(@Mode = 1)/*for normal form*/
	BEGIN
		UPDATE [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
		   SET [IsChecked] = 1
			  ,[CheckerUserId] = @CheckerUserId
			  ,[CheckedDate] = GETDATE()
			  ,[CheckedDatePersian] = @CheckedDatePersian
		WHERE [FormFieldMasterValueId] = @MasterValueId
		/**/
		UPDATE [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValueDetails]
		   SET [IsChecked] = 1
			  ,[CheckerUserId] = @CheckerUserId
			  ,[CheckedDate] = GETDATE()
			  ,[CheckedDatePersian] = @CheckedDatePersian
		WHERE [MasterValueId] = @MasterValueId
	END
	ELSE /*for form has a workflow*/
	BEGIN
		UPDATE [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
		   SET [IsChecked] = 1
			  ,[CheckerUserId] = @CheckerUserId
			  ,[CheckedDate] = GETDATE()
			  ,[CheckedDatePersian] = @CheckedDatePersian
		WHERE [FormFieldMasterValueId] = @MasterValueId	
		DECLARE @FormId INT
		SET  @FormId = (SELECT TOP 1 [FormId] FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues] WHERE [FormFieldMasterValueId] = @MasterValueId) 
		IF(ISNULL(@FormId,0) > 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			UPDATE [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValueDetails]
			   SET [IsChecked] = 1
				  ,[CheckerUserId] = @CheckerUserId
				  ,[CheckedDate] = GETDATE()
				  ,[CheckedDatePersian] = @CheckedDatePersian
			WHERE [MasterValueId] = @MasterValueId
			AND [FormId] = @FormId
		/**/
		END		
	END			
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[MobspDyFormCheckExit]





/*AI14010519 - Copied from web*/
/*AI14011003 - Set NULL CheckedDate*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspDyFormCheckExit]
(
/* 
	EXEC MobspDyFormCheckExit 361, '13970824',2
*/	
@MasterValueId INT,
@CheckedDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@Mode INT /*mode=1 ===> form is normal, mode=2 ===> form is a workflow*/
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
	IF(@Mode = 1)/*for normal form*/
	BEGIN
		UPDATE [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
		   SET [IsChecked] = 0
			  ,[CheckerUserId] = NULL
			  /*AI14011003*/
			  /*,[CheckedDate] = GETDATE()
			  ,[CheckedDatePersian] = @CheckedDatePersian*/
			  ,[CheckedDate] = NULL
			  ,[CheckedDatePersian] = NULL
			  /*AI14011003-End*/
		WHERE [FormFieldMasterValueId] = @MasterValueId
		/**/
		UPDATE [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValueDetails]
		   SET [IsChecked] = 0
			  ,[CheckerUserId] = NULL
			  /*AI14011003*/
			  /*,[CheckedDate] = GETDATE()
			  ,[CheckedDatePersian] = @CheckedDatePersian*/
			  ,[CheckedDate] = NULL
			  ,[CheckedDatePersian] = NULL
			  /*AI14011003-End*/
		WHERE [MasterValueId] = @MasterValueId
	END
	ELSE /*for form has a workflow*/
	BEGIN
		UPDATE [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
		   SET [IsChecked] = 0
			  ,[CheckerUserId] = NULL
			  /*AI14011003*/
			  /*,[CheckedDate] = GETDATE()
			  ,[CheckedDatePersian] = @CheckedDatePersian*/
			  ,[CheckedDate] = NULL
			  ,[CheckedDatePersian] = NULL
			  /*AI14011003-End*/
		WHERE [FormFieldMasterValueId] = @MasterValueId		
		DECLARE @FormId INT
		SET  @FormId = (SELECT TOP 1 [FormId] FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues] WHERE [FormFieldMasterValueId] = @MasterValueId) 
		IF(ISNULL(@FormId,0) > 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			UPDATE [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValueDetails]
		    SET [IsChecked] = 0
			  ,[CheckerUserId] = NULL
			  /*AI14011003*/
			  /*,[CheckedDate] = GETDATE()
			  ,[CheckedDatePersian] = @CheckedDatePersian*/
			  ,[CheckedDate] = NULL
			  ,[CheckedDatePersian] = NULL
			  /*AI14011003-End*/
			WHERE [MasterValueId] = @MasterValueId
			AND [FormId] = @FormId
		/**/
		END		
	END	
/**/
END

/*AI14011003*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspDyUpdateToDeleteFormFieldMasterValue]
(		
/*
	EXEC MobspDyUpdateToDeleteFormFieldMasterValue 30
*/
@MasterValueId INT
)
As
BEGIN
/**/
BEGIN TRY
    BEGIN TRANSACTION 
		UPDATE [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldValues]
		   SET [IsDeleted] = 1
		WHERE MasterValueId = @MasterValueId
		/**/
		UPDATE [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValueDetails]
		   SET [IsDeleted] = 1
		WHERE MasterValueId = @MasterValueId
		/**/
		UPDATE [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
		   SET [IsDeleted] = 1
		WHERE FormFieldMasterValueId = @MasterValueId
				
		SELECT 1 AS Result
    COMMIT
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK	
	SELECT 0 AS Result
END CATCH
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[MobspInsertCompanyChangeHistory]






/*AI14020705 - Copied from web*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspInsertCompanyChangeHistory]
/*
	declare @InsertedId int = 0
	exec MobspInsertCompanyChangeHistory 
	@CompanyId = 68697,
	@FieldName = N'cmpReferUserId',
	@OldValue = N'56',
	@NewValue = N'3',
	@Desc = N'', 
	@CmpChangeHistory_Lang = NULL, 	
	@UserId = 3,
	@RegisterDate = '2023-02-13',
	@RegisterDatePersian = '14011124',
	@AppType = 'dasti',
	@DefaultLanguageID = 1065,		
	@InsertedId = @InsertedId OUTPUT
	select @InsertedId 
*/
(
@CompanyId INT,
@FieldName NVARCHAR(300),
@OldValue NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
@NewValue NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
@Desc NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL, 
@CmpChangeHistory_Lang NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL, 
/**/
@UserId INT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@AppType NVARCHAR(20),
@DefaultLanguageID INT,
/**/
@InsertedId INT OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
	/*============================================================================*/
	/*================================ Insert ====================================*/
	INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompanyChangeHistory]
        ([CompanyID]
        ,[FieldName]
        ,[OldValue]
        ,[NewValue]
        ,[Desc]
        ,[UserId]
        ,[RegisterDate]
        ,[RegisterDatePersian]        
        ,[AppType]
        ,[LanguageID])
    VALUES
        (@CompanyID
        ,@FieldName
        ,@OldValue
        ,@NewValue
        ,@Desc
        ,@UserId
        ,@RegisterDate
        ,@RegisterDatePersian
        ,@AppType
        ,@DefaultLanguageID)
	SET @InsertedId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()	
	IF(ISNULL(@InsertedId,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@CmpChangeHistory_Lang,'') <> '')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompanyChangeHistory_Lang]
			([CompanyChangeHistoryId]
			,[LanguageID]
			,[OldValue]
			,[NewValue]
			,[Desc])
		SELECT 
			@InsertedId,
			JS.[LanguageID],
			JS.[OldValue],
			JS.[NewValue],
			JS.[Desc]
		FROM OPENJSON(ISNULL(@CmpChangeHistory_Lang,'[]'))
		WITH (
			[LanguageID] INT '$.LanguageID',
			[OldValue] NVARCHAR(MAX) '$.OldValue',
			[NewValue] NVARCHAR(MAX) '$.NewValue',
			[Desc] NVARCHAR(MAX) '$.Desc'
		) AS JS
		WHERE JS.[LanguageID] <> @DefaultLanguageID
		AND JS.[LanguageID] IN(SELECT L.[LanguageID]
							   FROM [dbo].[Languages] L
							   WHERE ISNULL(L.IsActive,0) = 1)
	/**/
	END		
	/*===========================================================================*/
/**/
END

/*RZ960928*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[MobspInsertProjectInformationsDefualtField]
(		
/*
	exec MobspInsertProjectInformationsDefualtField 
	@ProjectID = 6968,
	@UserId = 3,
	@Year1 = 2,
	@Month1 = 9,
	@RegisterDate = '2017-12-19',
	@RegisterDatePersian = '13960928',
	@AppType = 'dasti',
	@LanguageID = 1065,
	@FirstRegisterUserId  = 3,
	@FirstRegisterDatePersian = '13960928',
	@FirstRegisterDate = '2017-12-19' 
*/
@ProjectID INT,
@UserId INT,
@Year1 INT,
@Month1 INT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian VARCHAR(8),
@AppType NVARCHAR(50),
@LanguageID INT,
@FirstRegisterUserId INT,
@FirstRegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(10),
@FirstRegisterDate DATETIME 
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
	INSERT INTO dbo.CrmProjectInformations (
		ProjectId,
		FieldTitleId,
		UserId,
		Year1,
		Month1,
		RegisterDate,
		RegisterDatePersian,
		AppType, 
		LanguageID,
		FirstRegisterUserId,
		FirstRegisterDatePersian,
		FirstRegisterDate
	) 
	SELECT 
		@ProjectID,
		ProjectFieldTitleId,
		@UserId,
		@Year1,
		@Month1,
		GETDATE(),
		@RegisterDatePersian,
		@AppType, 
		@LanguageID,
		@FirstRegisterUserId,
		@FirstRegisterDatePersian,
		@FirstRegisterDate 
	FROM dbo.CrmProjectFieldTitle
	WHERE IsDefualtField = 1
	AND ProjectFieldTitleId NOT IN ( SELECT ProjectFieldTitleId 
					FROM CrmProjectFieldTitle
					WHERE ProjectId = @ProjectID)
/**/
END

		CompanyPersonId,

		@RegisterDatePersian,

		@SendTime, 
		@SendDateTime,
		@AppType,
		@SendPriority,
		@DefaultLanguageID,

		@RegisterDatePersian,
		GETDATE(), 
		@SendNeedCheck,

		@ObjectId,
		@ObjectIdType,
		@OTP
	FROM SmsTempMobileNumbers 
	WHERE UserId = @UserId 
	And Selector = 1	
	AND MobileLength = 11
	AND MobilePreNum = '09'

	--*/

-- [dbo].[MobspInternalMessageSendSingle]






/*RZ960929*/
/*RZ961002- add length*/
/*RZ14001207- Add fields*/
/*AI14011005 - Add wsDoc*/
/*AI14011221 - Add parameter*/
/*AI14020421 - Insert to UserPushNotifTank*/
/*AI14020918 - Add WSStockSupply*/
/*AI14021005 - Change IDENTITY*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspInternalMessageSendSingle]
(		
/*
	EXEC MobspInternalMessageSendSingle
*/
@msgMasterId INT = NULL,
@msgSubject NVARCHAR(MAX),
@msgBody NVARCHAR(MAX),
/*--------------*/
@msgdTypeId INT ,
@msgdFromUserId INT ,
@msgdToUserId INT ,
@msgdSendDate VARCHAR(8),
@msgdSendTime VARCHAR(20),
@msgdSendDateTime DATETIME,
@msgdPriorityId INT,
@msgdAttachFile NVARCHAR(200) = NULL,
@msgdAttachRecId INT = NULL,
@msgdAttachRecDesc NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
@msgdGroupId INT = NULL,
/*---------------*/
@UserId INT,
@Year1 SMALLINT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian VARCHAR(8),
@AppType NVARCHAR(50)
,@InsertedID INT OUTPUT 
,@LanguageID INT = NULL 
/*AI14011221*/
,@msgdAttachRecIdI INT = NULL
,@msgdAttachRecDescI NVARCHAR(100) = NULL
/*AI14011221-End*/
)
As
BEGIN
/**/
/*RZ961002- add length*/
/*================================= set subject ============================*/
IF(@msgSubject IS NOT NULL AND @msgSubject <> '')
BEGIN
	SET @msgSubject = LEFT (@msgSubject, 200)
END
/*------------------------------------------------------------------------------	
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
--********************MAINTRAN*/
/*------------- Set CompanyId And ProjectId -----------------*/
/*RZ14001207- Add fields*/
DECLARE @msgCompanyId INT,
@msgProjectId INT
IF(ISNULL(@msgdAttachRecId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
/**/
	IF(ISNULL(@msgdTypeId,0) IN(5,60)) /*PORD*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SELECT TOP 1
			@msgCompanyId = pordCompanyId,
			@msgProjectId = pordProjectId
		FROM WSPreOrder
		WHERE PreOrderID = @msgdAttachRecId
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(ISNULL(@msgdTypeId,0) = 8) /*ORD*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SELECT TOP 1
			@msgCompanyId = ordCompanyId,
			@msgProjectId = ordProjectId
		FROM WSOrder
		WHERE OrderID = @msgdAttachRecId
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(ISNULL(@msgdTypeId,0) = 9) /*BORD*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SELECT TOP 1
			@msgCompanyId = bordCompanyId,
			@msgProjectId = bordProjectId
		FROM WSBuyOrder
		WHERE BuyOrderID = @msgdAttachRecId
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(ISNULL(@msgdTypeId,0) in(10,45,50)) /*CNVS*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SELECT TOP 1
			@msgCompanyId = cnvsCompanyId,
			@msgProjectId = cnvsProjectId
		FROM CrmConversation
		WHERE ConversationID = @msgdAttachRecId
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(ISNULL(@msgdTypeId,0) = 14) /*LETT*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SELECT TOP 1
			@msgCompanyId = LetterCompanyId,
			@msgProjectId = LettercmpProjectId
		FROM SecLetters
		WHERE LetterId = @msgdAttachRecId
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(ISNULL(@msgdTypeId,0) IN(16,17,55)) /*DyForm*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SELECT TOP 1
			@msgCompanyId = CompanyId,
			@msgProjectId = ProjectId
		FROM CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues
		WHERE FormFieldMasterValueId = @msgdAttachRecId
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(ISNULL(@msgdTypeId,0) = 18) /*CTSH*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SELECT TOP 1
			@msgCompanyId = (SELECT TOP 1 CompanyID FROM CrmCompany WHERE CompanyID = 
											(SELECT prjCompanyId FROM CrmProjects WHERE ProjectID = [ctshCmpProjectId])),
			@msgProjectId = [ctshCmpProjectId]
		FROM [WSCostSheets]
		WHERE [CostSheetId] = @msgdAttachRecId
	/**/
	END	
	/*AI14011005- Remove Type 12 in internal message and add wsDoc*/
	ELSE IF(ISNULL(@msgdTypeId,0) = 20) /*DOC*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SELECT TOP 1
			@msgCompanyId = CompanyId,
			@msgProjectId = ProjectId
		FROM [dbo].[WSDoc]
		WHERE DocID = @msgdAttachRecId
	/**/
	END
	/*AI14011005- End*/
	/*AI14020918- Add WSStockSupply*/
	ELSE IF(ISNULL(@msgdTypeId,0) = 21) /*StockSupply*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SELECT TOP 1
			@msgCompanyId = stsupCompanyId,
			@msgProjectId = stsupProjectId
		FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupply]
		WHERE StockSupplyId = @msgdAttachRecId
	/**/
	END
	/*AI14020918- End*/
/**/
END
/*RZ14001207- End*/
/*----------------------------------------------------------*/
DECLARE @ErrorNumber INT, @ErrorFrom VARCHAR(50)
BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
/*--********************MAINTRAN
------------------- tran1*/
if (@LanguageID is null) SET @LanguageID = 1065
BEGIN TRAN
/*------------------- tran1*/
INSERT INTO dbo.CrmInternalMessage (
	msgMasterId,
	msgSubject,
	msgBody,
	msgFromUserId,
	UserId,
	Year1,
	Month1,
	RegisterDate,
	RegisterDatePersian, 
	AppType, 
	LanguageID, 
	FirstRegisterUserId,  
    FirstRegisterDatePersian,  
    FirstRegisterDate,
	/*RZ14001207- Add fields*/
	[msgCompanyId],
    [msgProjectId]
	/*RZ14001207- End*/
) VALUES ( 
	@msgMasterId ,
	@msgSubject ,
	@msgBody ,
	@msgdFromUserId,
	@UserId ,
	@Year1 ,
	@Month1 ,
	@RegisterDate ,
	@RegisterDatePersian, 
	@AppType,
	@LanguageID, 
	@UserId,  
	@RegisterDatePersian,  
	@RegisterDate ,

				(SELECT @MessageID AS 'msgId', @msgdTypeId AS 'msgdTypeId', @msgdFromUserId AS 'msgdFromUserId' FOR JSON PATH)
	SET @upExtraData = REPLACE(@upExtraData, '[', '')
	SET @upExtraData = REPLACE(@upExtraData, ']', '')
	/**/
	EXEC [dbo].[MobspInsertUserPushNotifTank]
		@upTypeId = 1
		,@upUserIds = @NotifUsers
		,@upTitle = @msgSubject
		,@upBody = NULL
		,@upExtraData = @upExtraData
		,@upPriority = NULL
		,@UserId = @UserId
		,@Year1 = @Year1
		,@Month1 = @Month1
		,@RegisterDate = @RegisterDate
		,@RegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian
		,@AppType = @AppType
		,@LanguageID = @LanguageID
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
END CATCH
/*AI14020421-End*/

begin
	RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)
	ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
	/*--Set @OrderId = 0
	--Return @OrderId*/
	SET @InsertedID = -1  
end
ELSE
BEGIN
	COMMIT TRANSACTION Main
	/*--Return @OrderId*/
	SET @InsertedID = @MessageID  
END

END

-- [dbo].[MobspIntMsgGetCmpVerifyMessageCnt]





/*AI14000609*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspIntMsgGetCmpVerifyMessageCnt]
(	
/* 
	EXEC MobspIntMsgGetCmpVerifyMessageCnt 3 
*/
@ToUserId INT,
@CompanyId INT
)
AS
BEGIN
/*--*/
	SELECT COUNT(*) Cnt 
	FROM dbo.CrmInternalMessageDetails MD 
	WHERE (CASE
		WHEN MD.msgdTypeId = 5 THEN(SELECT pordCompanyId FROM dbo.WSPreOrder WHERE PreOrderID = MD.msgdAttachRecId) 
		WHEN MD.msgdTypeId = 8 THEN(SELECT ordCompanyId FROM dbo.WSOrder WHERE OrderID = MD.msgdAttachRecId) 
		END) = @CompanyId
		AND msgdToUserId = @ToUserId
		AND (msgdIsRead = 0 OR msgdIsRead is NULL)
END

/*AI14000609*/
/*AI14000704 - Add field*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspIntMsgSelectCmpVerifyMessage]
(	
/* 
	EXEC MobspIntMsgSelectCmpVerifyMessage 8020, -20, 1, 10, false ,1065, 1065
*/
@CmpId INT,
@ToUserId INT,
@PageNumber INT,
@PageSize INT,
@IsRead BIT = NULL,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL 
)
AS
BEGIN
	IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
	BEGIN
		IF(@IsRead IS NULL)
		BEGIN
			;WITH CtePaging
			 AS (
					SELECT 
						CAST(COUNT(*) OVER() AS BIGINT) AS TotalCount,
						Row_number() OVER(ORDER BY msgdSendDateTime DESC, msgdPriorityId) AS rownum
						,M.InternalMessageID
						,M.msgSubject
						,MD.msgdTypeId
						,(SELECT MessageType FROM dbo.CrmInternalMessageType WHERE InternalMessageTypeID = MD.msgdTypeId) MessageType
						,MD.msgdOldTypeId
						,MD.InternalMessageDetailID
						,MD.msgdFromUserId
						,(SELECT ISNULL(FName, '') + ' '+ ISNULL(LName, '') FROM dbo.Users WHERE UserId = MD.msgdFromUserId) FromUser
						,MD.msgdToUserId
						,(SELECT ISNULL(FName, '') + ' '+ ISNULL(LName, '') FROM dbo.Users WHERE UserId = MD.msgdToUserId) ToUser
						,MD.msgdSendDateTime
						,MD.msgdReceiveDateTime
						,MD.msgdIsRead
						,MD.msgdReadDateTime /*AI14000704*/
						,MD.msgdPriorityId
						,(SELECT InternalMessagePriority FROM dbo.CrmInternalMessagePriority WHERE InternalMessagePriorityID = MD.msgdPriorityId) InternalMessagePriority
						,MD.msgdGroupId
						,M.UserId
						,ISNULL(M.msgMasterId, InternalMessageID) msgMasterId
						,M.msgIsFinalised
						,M.msgLastGroupId
						,M.msgActiveInCycle   
					 FROM dbo.CrmInternalMessage M LEFT JOIN dbo.CrmInternalMessageDetails MD 
							ON(M.InternalMessageID = MD.msgdInternalMessageID)
					 WHERE (CASE
						WHEN MD.msgdTypeId = 5 THEN(SELECT pordCompanyId FROM dbo.WSPreOrder WHERE PreOrderID = MD.msgdAttachRecId) 
						WHEN MD.msgdTypeId = 8 THEN(SELECT ordCompanyId FROM dbo.WSOrder WHERE OrderID = MD.msgdAttachRecId) 
						END) = @cmpID
						AND msgdToUserId = @ToUserId
				) 
			/**/	
			SELECT 
				* ,
				(SELECT 
					CASE  WHEN (Count(*) - ((Count(*) / @PageSize) * @PageSize) ) = 0					
					THEN
						Count(*) / @PageSize
					ELSE
						(Count(*) / @PageSize) + 1
					END
				FROM CtePaging) AS AllPage
			FROM   CtePaging 
			WHERE  rownum BETWEEN ( @PageNumber - 1 ) * @PageSize + 1 AND @PageNumber * @PageSize
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			;WITH CtePaging
			AS (
				SELECT 
					CAST(COUNT(*) OVER() AS BIGINT) AS TotalCount,
					Row_number() OVER(ORDER BY msgdSendDateTime DESC, msgdPriorityId) AS rownum
					,M.InternalMessageID
					,M.msgSubject
					,MD.msgdTypeId
					,(SELECT MessageType FROM dbo.CrmInternalMessageType WHERE InternalMessageTypeID = MD.msgdTypeId) MessageType
					,MD.msgdOldTypeId
					,MD.InternalMessageDetailID
					,MD.msgdFromUserId
					,(SELECT ISNULL(FName, '') + ' '+ ISNULL(LName, '') FROM dbo.Users WHERE UserId = MD.msgdFromUserId) FromUser
					,MD.msgdToUserId
					,(SELECT ISNULL(FName, '') + ' '+ ISNULL(LName, '') FROM dbo.Users WHERE UserId = MD.msgdToUserId) ToUser
					,MD.msgdSendDateTime
					,MD.msgdReceiveDateTime
					,MD.msgdIsRead
					,MD.msgdReadDateTime /*AI14000704*/
					,MD.msgdPriorityId
					,(SELECT InternalMessagePriority FROM dbo.CrmInternalMessagePriority WHERE InternalMessagePriorityID = MD.msgdPriorityId) InternalMessagePriority
					,MD.msgdGroupId
					,M.UserId
					,ISNULL(M.msgMasterId, InternalMessageID) msgMasterId
					,M.msgIsFinalised
					,M.msgLastGroupId
					,M.msgActiveInCycle   
				 FROM dbo.CrmInternalMessage M LEFT JOIN dbo.CrmInternalMessageDetails MD 
						ON(M.InternalMessageID = MD.msgdInternalMessageID)
				 WHERE (CASE
					WHEN MD.msgdTypeId = 5 THEN(SELECT pordCompanyId FROM dbo.WSPreOrder WHERE PreOrderID = MD.msgdAttachRecId) 
					WHEN MD.msgdTypeId = 8 THEN(SELECT ordCompanyId FROM dbo.WSOrder WHERE OrderID = MD.msgdAttachRecId) 
					END) = @cmpID
					AND msgdToUserId = @ToUserId
					AND MD.msgdIsRead = @IsRead
			) 
		/**/	
		SELECT 
			* , 
			(SELECT 
				CASE  WHEN (Count(*) - ((Count(*) / @PageSize) * @PageSize) ) = 0					
				THEN
					Count(*) / @PageSize
				ELSE
					(Count(*) / @PageSize) + 1
				END
			FROM CtePaging) AS AllPage
		FROM   CtePaging 
		WHERE  rownum BETWEEN ( @PageNumber - 1 ) * @PageSize + 1 AND @PageNumber * @PageSize
		END
	END
	ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID) 
	BEGIN
		IF(@IsRead IS NULL)
		BEGIN
			;WITH CtePaging
			 AS (
					SELECT 
						CAST(COUNT(*) OVER() AS BIGINT) AS TotalCount,
						Row_number() OVER(ORDER BY msgdSendDateTime DESC, msgdPriorityId) AS rownum
						,M.InternalMessageID
						,(SELECT msgSubject FROM CrmInternalMessage_Lang lng WHERE lng.InternalMessageID = M.InternalMessageID AND lng.LanguageID = @LanguageID) AS [msgSubject]
						,MD.msgdTypeId
						,(SELECT MessageType FROM dbo.CrmInternalMessageType_Lang lnt WHERE lnt.InternalMessageTypeID = MD.msgdTypeId AND lnt.LanguageID = @LanguageID) AS [MessageType]
						,MD.msgdOldTypeId
						,MD.InternalMessageDetailID
						,MD.msgdFromUserId
						,(SELECT ISNULL(FName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(LName, '') FROM Users_Lang u WHERE u.UserId = MD.msgdFromUserId AND u.LanguageID = @LanguageID) FromUser
						,MD.msgdToUserId
						,(SELECT ISNULL(FName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(LName, '') FROM Users_Lang u WHERE u.UserId = MD.msgdToUserId AND u.LanguageID = @LanguageID) ToUser
						,MD.msgdSendDateTime
						,MD.msgdReceiveDateTime
						,MD.msgdIsRead
						,MD.msgdReadDateTime /*AI14000704*/
						,MD.msgdPriorityId
						,(SELECT InternalMessagePriority FROM dbo.CrmInternalMessagePriority_Lang lnt WHERE lnt.InternalMessagePriorityID = MD.msgdPriorityId AND lnt.LanguageID = @LanguageID) AS [InternalMessagePriority]
						,MD.msgdGroupId
						,M.UserId
						,ISNULL(M.msgMasterId, InternalMessageID) msgMasterId
						,M.msgIsFinalised
						,M.msgLastGroupId
						,M.msgActiveInCycle
					 FROM dbo.CrmInternalMessage M LEFT JOIN dbo.CrmInternalMessageDetails MD 
							ON(M.InternalMessageID = MD.msgdInternalMessageID)
					 WHERE (CASE
						WHEN MD.msgdTypeId = 5 THEN(SELECT pordCompanyId FROM dbo.WSPreOrder WHERE PreOrderID = MD.msgdAttachRecId) 
						WHEN MD.msgdTypeId = 8 THEN(SELECT ordCompanyId FROM dbo.WSOrder WHERE OrderID = MD.msgdAttachRecId) 
						END) = @cmpID
						AND msgdToUserId = @ToUserId
				) 
			/**/	
			SELECT 
				* ,
				(SELECT 
					CASE  WHEN (Count(*) - ((Count(*) / @PageSize) * @PageSize) ) = 0					
					THEN
						Count(*) / @PageSize
					ELSE
						(Count(*) / @PageSize) + 1
					END
				FROM CtePaging) AS AllPage
			FROM   CtePaging 
			WHERE  rownum BETWEEN ( @PageNumber - 1 ) * @PageSize + 1 AND @PageNumber * @PageSize
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			;WITH CtePaging
			 AS (
					SELECT 
						CAST(COUNT(*) OVER() AS BIGINT) AS TotalCount,
						Row_number() OVER(ORDER BY msgdSendDateTime DESC, msgdPriorityId) AS rownum
						,M.InternalMessageID
						,(SELECT msgSubject FROM CrmInternalMessage_Lang lng WHERE lng.InternalMessageID = M.InternalMessageID AND lng.LanguageID = @LanguageID) AS [msgSubject]
						,MD.msgdTypeId
						,(SELECT MessageType FROM dbo.CrmInternalMessageType_Lang lnt WHERE lnt.InternalMessageTypeID = MD.msgdTypeId AND lnt.LanguageID = @LanguageID) AS [MessageType]
						,MD.msgdOldTypeId
						,MD.InternalMessageDetailID
						,MD.msgdFromUserId
						,(SELECT ISNULL(FName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(LName, '') FROM Users_Lang u WHERE u.UserId = MD.msgdFromUserId AND u.LanguageID = @LanguageID) FromUser
						,MD.msgdToUserId
						,(SELECT ISNULL(FName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(LName, '') FROM Users_Lang u WHERE u.UserId = MD.msgdToUserId AND u.LanguageID = @LanguageID) ToUser
						,MD.msgdSendDateTime
						,MD.msgdReceiveDateTime
						,MD.msgdIsRead
						,MD.msgdReadDateTime /*AI14000704*/
						,MD.msgdPriorityId
						,(SELECT InternalMessagePriority FROM dbo.CrmInternalMessagePriority_Lang lnt WHERE lnt.InternalMessagePriorityID = MD.msgdPriorityId AND lnt.LanguageID = @LanguageID) AS [InternalMessagePriority]
						,MD.msgdGroupId
						,M.UserId
						,ISNULL(M.msgMasterId, InternalMessageID) msgMasterId
						,M.msgIsFinalised
						,M.msgLastGroupId
						,M.msgActiveInCycle
					 FROM dbo.CrmInternalMessage M LEFT JOIN dbo.CrmInternalMessageDetails MD 
							ON(M.InternalMessageID = MD.msgdInternalMessageID)
					 WHERE (CASE
						WHEN MD.msgdTypeId = 5 THEN(SELECT pordCompanyId FROM dbo.WSPreOrder WHERE PreOrderID = MD.msgdAttachRecId) 
						WHEN MD.msgdTypeId = 8 THEN(SELECT ordCompanyId FROM dbo.WSOrder WHERE OrderID = MD.msgdAttachRecId) 
						END) = @cmpID
						AND msgdToUserId = @ToUserId
						AND MD.msgdIsRead = @IsRead
				) 
			/**/	
			SELECT 
				* , 
				(SELECT 
					CASE  WHEN (Count(*) - ((Count(*) / @PageSize) * @PageSize) ) = 0					
					THEN
						Count(*) / @PageSize
					ELSE
						(Count(*) / @PageSize) + 1
					END
				FROM CtePaging) AS AllPage
			FROM   CtePaging 
			WHERE  rownum BETWEEN ( @PageNumber - 1 ) * @PageSize + 1 AND @PageNumber * @PageSize
		END
	END
END

/*AI14020518 - Copied from web*/
/*AI14020912 - Not Add Product by prdKind 2 - Calculate RateResult in fiexd PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto*/
/*RZ14021202- Add OrderItemId to WSDocItems*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspIssueDraftFromOrder]
(		
/*
	declare @InsertedDraftID INT = 0, @InsertedDraftNumber INT = 0
	EXEC MobspIssueDraftFromOrder  
	@OrderID = 17613,
	@DraftDate = '14000226',
	@UserId  = 3,
	@Month1 = 2,
	@FPId = 30,
	@WHId = 1,
	@RegisterDatePersian = '14000226',
	@AppType = 'dasti',
	@InsertedDraftID = @InsertedDraftID OUTPUT ,
	@InsertedDraftNumber = @InsertedDraftNumber OUTPUT,	
	@LanguageID = 1065,
	@OrdItemList = N'3113,5|3114,3|3115,2|3116,0|3117,4|3118,4|3120,0'
	select @InsertedDraftID,@InsertedDraftNumber
	 
*/
@OrderID INT,
@DraftDate VARCHAR(8),
@UserId INT,
@Month1 INT,
@FPId INT,
@WHId INT,
@RegisterDatePersian VARCHAR(8),
@AppType NVARCHAR(50),
@InsertedDraftID INT OUTPUT ,
@InsertedDraftNumber INT OUTPUT,
@LanguageID INT ,
@OrdItemList NVARCHAR(MAX)
)
As
BEGIN
/**/
/*---------------------- Set TmpTabel -------------------------------------*/
DECLARE @TmpTable TABLE (
	[OrderItemId] INT,
	[Quantity] FLOAT
)
IF(ISNULL(@OrdItemList,'') <> '')
BEGIN
/**/
	DECLARE @CrsrOrderItemId INT,@CrsrQuantity FLOAT,@Text NVARCHAR(MAX)
	/**/
	DECLARE CrsrI CURSOR LOCAL FOR
	/**/	
		SELECT SplitData  
		FROM fnSplitString(@OrdItemList, '|')
	/**/
	OPEN CrsrI
	FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrI INTO @Text
	WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
	BEGIN
	/**/
		/*================================================================================*/
		DECLARE @Field NVARCHAR(MAX) = ''
		SET @CrsrOrderItemId = NULL
		SET @CrsrQuantity = NULL			
		/**/
		/*****************************************************************/
		IF(ISNULL(@Text,'') <> '') 
		BEGIN
		/**/
			/*===========================================================*/	
			SET @CrsrOrderItemId = CAST(ISNULL(SUBSTRING(@Text, 1, CHARINDEX(',',@Text)-1),'0') AS INT)                                    
			SET @CrsrQuantity = CAST(ISNULL(SUBSTRING(@Text, CHARINDEX(',', @Text)+1, Len(@Text)),'0') AS FLOAT)

			INSERT INTO @TmpTable ([OrderItemId],[Quantity])
			VALUES(@CrsrOrderItemId,@CrsrQuantity)
								
			/*===========================================================*/				
		/**/
		END
		/*================================================================================*/					    				 					
	/**/	
	FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrI INTO @Text
	END
	CLOSE CrsrI
	DEALLOCATE CrsrI
/**/
END

/*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
DECLARE @InsertedOldDraftID INT
/*���� ���� ���� � �ǘ��� ����� �� ����� ���� �ѐ� ����� ���� ����  �� ������ ������ ����� �����  ����� �� ���.
 ���� �� ���� �ѐ� ����� �� ����� ������ ���� �� �ǘ��� ���� ���.*/
IF(ISNULL((
	ISNULL((SELECT SUM(ISNULL(ordIQuantity,0))
			FROM dbo.WSOrderItems
			WHERE OrderId = @OrderID
			/*AI14020912- Not Add Product by prdKind 2*/
			AND ordIPrdId NOT IN(SELECT ProductID 
								FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] 
								WHERE ISNULL(PrdKind,1) = 2 )
			/*AI14020912- End*/
			),0)
	-
    ISNULL((SELECT SUM(ISNULL(Quantity,0)) FROM dbo.WSDocItems DI WHERE DI.DocId IN(SELECT D.DocId FROM [dbo].[WSDoc] D WHERE D.OrderId = @OrderID)),0)
	),0) = 0
)
BEGIN
	RETURN -1
END
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
DECLARE @DocDateTime DATETIME, @TimeNow TIME
SET @DocDateTime = (SELECT dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@DraftDate, null))
SET @TimeNow = CONVERT(TIME, GETDATE())
SET @DocDateTime = @DocDateTime + CAST(@TimeNow AS DATETIME)
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
DECLARE @DraftNumber INT
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
DECLARE @HavePermissionMoveOrdDescIIToDoc INT
IF EXISTS (
SELECT * FROM dbo.CrmsysRollPermission
WHERE FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmIssueDraftFromOrder' )
AND FormActionId = (SELECT Id FROM dbo.CrmsysFormActions WHERE ActionName = N'actMoveOrdDescriptionIIToDoc'
						AND FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmIssueDraftFromOrder' ))
AND RollId = (SELECT TOP 1 RollId FROM dbo.UsersPermission WHERE UserId = @UserId AND PrjId = 11/*CRM*/))
	SET @HavePermissionMoveOrdDescIIToDoc = 1
ELSE
	SET @HavePermissionMoveOrdDescIIToDoc = 0
	
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
/*--********************MAINTRAN*/
DECLARE @ErrorNumber INT, @ErrorFrom VARCHAR(50)
Declare @DraftId INT 
BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*--------------------------- MAIN TRANSACTION START ------------------------*/
/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
	/*--------------------------------------------*/
	DECLARE @WareHouseId INT
	DECLARE Crsr_Wrhs CURSOR LOCAL FOR				
		SELECT DISTINCT 
			ordiWHId
		FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems]
		WHERE OrderId = @OrderID 
		AND OrderItemID IN(SELECT TT.OrderItemId FROM @TmpTable TT WHERE ISNULL(TT.Quantity,0) > 0)
		/*AI14020912- Not Add Product by prdKind 2*/
		AND ordIPrdId NOT IN(SELECT ProductID 
							FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] 
							WHERE ISNULL(PrdKind,1) = 2 )
		/*AI14020912- End*/
	OPEN Crsr_Wrhs
	FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Wrhs INTO @WareHouseId
	WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
	BEGIN		
		SET @DraftId = NULL
		SET @InsertedDraftID = NULL
		SET @DraftNumber = NULL
		SET @InsertedDraftNumber = NULL
		/*---------------- Doc Number ----------------*/
		SELECT @DraftNumber =  dbo.WSfnReturnDocNumber(
								(SELECT DocOprId FROM dbo.WSOrderOpr op WHERE op.OrderOprID = 
								(SELECT OrderOprId FROM dbo.WSOrder WHERE OrderID = @OrderId) ) )
		SET @InsertedDraftNumber = @DraftNumber
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		/*--------------------- tran1 ----------------*/
		BEGIN TRAN
		/*------------------- tran1*/
		IF @HavePermissionMoveOrdDescIIToDoc = 1
		BEGIN
		/*---------------------------------*/
			INSERT INTO dbo.WSDoc (
				OprId,
				DocNumber,
				DocDate,
				DocDateTime,
				OrderId,
				/*--BuyOrderId,*/
				SendAddress, 
				IsFix,	
				WHId,
				UserId,	
				Month1,	
				FPId, 
				RegisterDate, 
				RegisterDatePersian, 
				FirstRegisterUserId, 
				FirstRegisterDate, 
				CompanyId,
				CompanyPersonId,
				ProjectId, 
				AppType,
				LanguageID,
				FirstRegisterDatePersian 
				,[Description]
			) 	
			SELECT  
				(SELECT DocOprId FROM dbo.WSOrderOpr op WHERE op.OrderOprID = O.OrderOprID), 
				@DraftNumber,
				@DraftDate,
				/*GETDATE(),*/
				@DocDateTime,
				OrderId,
				(SELECT City FROM dbo.City WHERE CityID = 
					(SELECT cmpCityId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = ordCompanyId)) 
				+ ' '+
				(SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province WHERE ProvinceId = 
					(SELECT cmpProvinceId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = ordCompanyId)) 
				+ ' '+
				(SELECT cmpAddress FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = ordCompanyId), 
				1,
				/*@WhID,*/
				ISNULL(@WareHouseId,@WhID),
				@UserId, 
				@Month1, 
				@FPId, 
				GETDATE(), 
				@RegisterDatePersian, 
				@UserId,
				GETDATE(), 
				ordCompanyId,
				ordCompanyPersonId,
				ordProjectId, 
				@AppType,
				@LanguageID, 
				@RegisterDatePersian
				,[ordDescriptionII]		 
			FROM dbo.WSOrder O 
			WHERE OrderId = @OrderId
			/*-------------- Begin insert to lang table ---------*/
			SELECT @DraftId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSDoc_Lang]
				(
				   [DocID]
				   ,[LanguageID]
				   ,[SendAddress]
				   ,[Description]
				 )
			SELECT 
				@DraftId,
				OL.LanguageID,
				(SELECT City FROM dbo.City_Lang CYLN WHERE CYLN.CityID = 
					(SELECT cmpCityId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = O.ordCompanyId)  AND CYLN.LanguageID = OL.LanguageID) 
				+ ' '+
				(SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province_Lang PLN WHERE PLN.ProvinceId = 
					(SELECT cmpProvinceId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = O.ordCompanyId) AND PLN.LanguageID = OL.LanguageID) 
				+ ' '+
				(SELECT cmpAddress FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CCLN WHERE CCLN.CompanyId = O.ordCompanyId AND CCLN.LanguageID = OL.LanguageID)
				,OL.[ordDescriptionII]
			FROM dbo.WSOrder_Lang OL 
			INNER JOIN dbo.WSOrder O ON OL.OrderId = O.OrderId
			WHERE O.OrderId = @OrderId
			/*-------------- End insert to lang table -----------*/ 
		/*---------------------------------*/
		END
		ELSE IF @HavePermissionMoveOrdDescIIToDoc = 0
		BEGIN
		/*---------------------------------*/
			INSERT INTO dbo.WSDoc (
				OprId,
				DocNumber,
				DocDate,
				DocDateTime,
				OrderId,
				/*--BuyOrderId,*/
				SendAddress, 
				IsFix,	
				WHId,
				UserId,	
				Month1,	
				FPId, 
				RegisterDate, 
				RegisterDatePersian, 
				FirstRegisterUserId, 
				FirstRegisterDate, 
				CompanyId,
				CompanyPersonId,
				ProjectId, 
				AppType,
				LanguageID,
				FirstRegisterDatePersian 
			) 	
			SELECT  
				(SELECT DocOprId FROM dbo.WSOrderOpr op WHERE op.OrderOprID = O.OrderOprID), 
				@DraftNumber,
				@DraftDate,
				/*GETDATE(),*/
				@DocDateTime,
				OrderId,
				(SELECT City FROM dbo.City WHERE CityID = 
					(SELECT cmpCityId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = ordCompanyId)) 
				+ ' '+
				(SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province WHERE ProvinceId = 
					(SELECT cmpProvinceId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = ordCompanyId)) 
				+ ' '+
				(SELECT cmpAddress FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = ordCompanyId), 
				1,
				/*@WhID,*/
				ISNULL(@WareHouseId,@WhID),
				@UserId, 
				@Month1, 
				@FPId, 
				GETDATE(), 
				@RegisterDatePersian, 
				@UserId,
				GETDATE(), 
				ordCompanyId,
				ordCompanyPersonId,
				ordProjectId, 
				@AppType,
				@LanguageID, 
				@RegisterDatePersian		 
			FROM dbo.WSOrder O 
			WHERE OrderId = @OrderId
			/*-------------- Begin insert to lang table ---------*/
			SELECT @DraftId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSDoc_Lang]
				(
				   [DocID]
				   ,[LanguageID]
				   ,[SendAddress]
				 )
			SELECT 
				@DraftId,
				OL.LanguageID,
				(SELECT City FROM dbo.City_Lang CYLN WHERE CYLN.CityID = 
					(SELECT cmpCityId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = O.ordCompanyId)  AND CYLN.LanguageID = OL.LanguageID) 
				+ ' '+
				(SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province_Lang PLN WHERE PLN.ProvinceId = 
					(SELECT cmpProvinceId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = O.ordCompanyId) AND PLN.LanguageID = OL.LanguageID) 
				+ ' '+
				(SELECT cmpAddress FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CCLN WHERE CCLN.CompanyId = O.ordCompanyId AND CCLN.LanguageID = OL.LanguageID)
			FROM dbo.WSOrder_Lang OL 
			INNER JOIN dbo.WSOrder O ON OL.OrderId = O.OrderId
			WHERE O.OrderId = @OrderId
			/*-------------- End insert to lang table -----------*/ 
		/*---------------------------------*/
		END
		/*------------------- tran1 ------------------------*/
		IF (@@ERROR > 0)
		BEGIN 
			SET @ErrorFrom='Insert Into WSDoc' 
			SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR 
		ROLLBACK TRAN 
		END		
		ELSE 
		COMMIT TRAN 
		/*------------------- tran1 ------------------------
		--===================================================================
		--===================================================================
		--===================================================================*/
		SET @InsertedDraftID = @DraftId
		/*------------------- tran2 --------------------------*/
		BEGIN TRAN
		/*---------------------------------------------*/
			DECLARE @OrderItemID INT = NULL, @ordIPrdId INT = NULL, @ordIQuantity float = NULL
			,@ordIDescription NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL, @ordIWHId INT = NULL
			,@ordIFCR FLOAT = NULL,@ordIFCRI FLOAT = NULL
			,@ordITracing NVARCHAR(200) = NULL
			,@ordIPrdUnitId INT = NULL
			,@ordIQuantityTotal FLOAT = NULL
			 ,@ordIPrdUnitIdI INT = NULL,@ordIPrdUnitIRateResult FLOAT = NULL
			 ,@PrdUnitIRate FLOAT = NULL,@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto BIT,@PrdQtyNoFCR BIT  = NULL
			, @TmpI FLOAT = NULL
			 ,@ordIPrdNickname NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL
			/*--*/
			DECLARE crsr CURSOR LOCAL FOR /*--For OrderItems*/
				SELECT 	
					OrderItemID,
					ordIPrdId,
					ordIQuantity,
					ordIDescription,
					ordIFCR, ordIFCRI,
					ordITracing
					,ordIPrdUnitId
					,ordIQuantityTotal
					,ordIPrdUnitIdI
					,ordIPrdUnitIRateResult
					,ordIPrdNickname
				FROM dbo.WSOrderItems
				WHERE OrderId = @OrderId
				AND ordIWHId = @WareHouseId
				/*AI14020912- Not Add Product by prdKind 2*/
				AND ordIPrdId NOT IN(SELECT ProductID 
								  FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] 
								  WHERE ISNULL(PrdKind,1) = 2 )
				/*AI14020912- End*/
			/*--*/
			OPEN crsr
			FETCH NEXT FROM crsr INTO @OrderItemID, @ordIPrdId, @ordIQuantity, @ordIDescription,
									  @ordIFCR, @ordIFCRI,
									  @ordITracing
									  ,@ordIPrdUnitId
									  ,@ordIQuantityTotal
									  ,@ordIPrdUnitIdI,@ordIPrdUnitIRateResult
									  ,@ordIPrdNickname
			/*--*/
			WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0 
			BEGIN
			/*---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
			/*------------------------- WSDocItems --------------------------------*/
			/*---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
				IF(EXISTS(SELECT * FROM @TmpTable))
				BEGIN
				/**/
					DECLARE @CurrQty FLOAT = NULL
					SET @CurrQty = (SELECT TOP 1 [Quantity] FROM @TmpTable WHERE OrderItemId = @OrderItemID)
					IF(ISNULL(@CurrQty,0) <= 0)
					BEGIN
					/**/	
						FETCH NEXT FROM crsr INTO @OrderItemID, @ordIPrdId, @ordIQuantity, @ordIDescription,
												  @ordIFCR, @ordIFCRI, @ordITracing,@ordIPrdUnitId
												  ,@ordIQuantityTotal
												  ,@ordIPrdUnitIdI,@ordIPrdUnitIRateResult
												  ,@ordIPrdNickname
						CONTINUE
					/**/
					END
					ELSE /*IF(ISNULL(@CurrQty,0) > 0)*/
					BEGIN
					/**/						
						IF(ISNULL(@ordIQuantityTotal,0) <> 0 AND ISNULL(@ordIQuantity,0) <> ISNULL(@CurrQty,0))
						BEGIN
						/**/
							SET @ordIQuantityTotal = ISNULL(@CurrQty,0) * ISNULL(@ordIFCR,1)
						/**/
						END
						SET @ordIQuantity = @CurrQty
					/**/
					END
				/**/
				END
				/*---------------------------------------------------------------*/
				SET @PrdUnitIRate = NULL
				SET @PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto = NULL
				SET @PrdQtyNoFCR = NULL	
				SET @TmpI = NULL
				/*--------------*/			
				SELECT 				
					@PrdUnitIRate = [PrdUnitIRate],
					@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto = [PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto],
					@PrdQtyNoFCR = [PrdQtyNoFCR]				
				FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]
				WHERE ProductId = @ordIPrdId
				/*--------------*/
				SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@ordIQuantity,1) * ISNULL(@ordIFCR,1) * ISNULL(@ordIFCRI,1)
				IF(ISNULL(@PrdQtyNoFCR,0) = 1)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@ordIQuantity,1) 					
				/**/
				END
				/*----------------------------------------------------*/
				IF(/*ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto,0) = 1 AND*/ ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRate,0) <> 0  AND ISNULL(@ordIPrdUnitIRateResult,0) <> 0) /*AI14020912- Calculate RateResult in fiexd PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto*/
				BEGIN
				/**/
					/*SET @ordIPrdUnitIRateResult = (1 / ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRate,0)) * ISNULL(@TmpI,1)*/
					SET @ordIPrdUnitIRateResult = (1 / ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRate,0)) * ISNULL(@ordIQuantity,1)

					IF(ISNULL(@ordIPrdUnitIRateResult,0) <> 0)
					BEGIN
					/**/
						SET @ordIPrdUnitIRateResult = CAST((FORMAT(@ordIPrdUnitIRateResult, '000.000')) AS FLOAT) 
					/**/
					END
				/**/
				END
				/*---------------------------------------------------------------*/
				INSERT INTO dbo.WSDocItems (
					DocId,
					PrdId,
					Quantity,
					[Description],
					UserId,	Month1,	FPId, RegisterDate, RegisterDatePersian,
					WHId, AppType,
					LanguageID,
					FirstRegisterDatePersian,
					FirstRegisterUserId,
					FirstRegisterDate
					,[FCR], [FCRI]
					,[Tracing]
					,[PrdUnitId]
					,[QuantityTotal]
					,[PrdUnitIdI]
					,[PrdUnitIRateResult]
					,[PrdNickname]
					,[OrderItemId] /*RZ14021202- Add OrderItemId to WSDocItems*/
				) 
				VALUES(
					@DraftId,
					@ordIPrdId,
					@ordIQuantity,
					@ordIDescription,
					@UserId, @Month1, @FPId, GETDATE(), @RegisterDatePersian,
					/*@WhID,*/
					ISNULL(@WareHouseId,@WhID),
					@AppType,
					@LanguageID,
					@RegisterDatePersian,
					@UserId,
					GETDATE()
					,@ordIFCR, @ordIFCRI
					,@ordITracing
					,@ordIPrdUnitId
					,@ordIQuantityTotal
					,@ordIPrdUnitIdI
					,@ordIPrdUnitIRateResult
					,@ordIPrdNickname
					,@OrderItemID /*RZ14021202- Add OrderItemId to WSDocItems*/
					)
				/*--*/
				DECLARE @DocItemId INT = NULL
				SELECT @DocItemId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
				INSERT INTO dbo.WSDocItems_Lang (
					[DocItemID]
				   ,[LanguageID]
				   ,[Description]	
				   ,[PrdNickname]
				) 
				SELECT
					@DocItemId,
					OILN.LanguageID,
					OILN.ordIDescription,
					OILN.ordIPrdNickname 
				FROM dbo.WSOrderItems_Lang OILN 
				WHERE  OILN.OrderItemID = @OrderItemID
				/*End insert records in lang table*/
				/*---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
				/*------------------------- WSDocItemSerials --------------------------*/
				/*---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
				/*------------------- tran3 ---------------------*/
				BEGIN TRAN
				/*------------------- tran3 ---------------------*/
					/*----------------- Begin user cursor for insert serial in doc -------------------*/
					/*-------------------------- Begin define variables ------------------------------*/
					DECLARE @SerialId INT = NULL
					DECLARE @ordItemSerialDescription NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
					DECLARE @OrderItemSerialID INT = NULL
					DECLARE @docItemSerialId INT = NULL
					/*-------------------------- End define variables --------------------------------*/
					DECLARE Crsr_Add_Serial CURSOR LOCAL For
						SELECT  TOP (CAST(@ordIQuantity AS INT))
							SerialId,
							[Description],
							OrderItemSerialID	
						FROM dbo.WSOrderItemSerials OSI
						WHERE OrderItemID = @OrderItemID
						AND SerialId NOT IN(SELECT   DIS.SerialId
											FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials] DIS
											WHERE [ItemId] IN (SELECT [DocItemID]    
																FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems]
																WHERE PrdId = @ordIPrdId
																AND [DocId] IN(SELECT D.DocId 
																				FROM [dbo].[WSDoc] D 
																				WHERE D.OrderId = @OrderId
																				AND D.OprId IN (SELECT DocOprID FROM dbo.WSDocOpr WHERE OprType IN(2,3)))
										
						
															 )											
											)
						AND  (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT DISI.Serial FROM dbo.WSDocItemSerials DISI WHERE DISI.DocItemSerialID = OSI.SerialId),'') AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) 
											NOT IN(SELECT (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(DIS.Serial AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
													FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials] DIS
													WHERE [ItemId] IN (SELECT [DocItemID]    
																		FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems]
																		WHERE PrdId = @ordIPrdId
																		AND [DocId] IN(SELECT D.DocId 
																						FROM [dbo].[WSDoc] D 
																						WHERE D.OrderId = @OrderId
																						AND D.OprId IN (SELECT DocOprID FROM dbo.WSDocOpr WHERE OprType = 1))																
																		)																
								)
						
					OPEN Crsr_Add_Serial
					FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Serial INTO @SerialId, @ordItemSerialDescription, @OrderItemSerialID
					WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
					BEGIN
					/*---------------------------------------*/
						DECLARE @Serial NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,@RootId INT = NULL,@OprType INT = NULL
						/*Find doc type via orderOpr*/
						SET @OprType = (SELECT TOP 1[OprType]
											FROM [dbo].[WSDocOpr]  DO
											WHERE DO.[DocOprID]  = ((SELECT  
																		(SELECT TOP 1 Opr.DocOprId 
																			FROM [dbo].[WSOrderOpr] Opr WHERE Opr.OrderOprID = O.OrderOprID) 			 
																		FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] O 
																		WHERE OrderId = @OrderId))						
										)
						/*check doc type*/
						IF(ISNULL(@OprType,0) = 1)/*Doc*/
						BEGIN
						/**/
							SELECT TOP 1 
								 @Serial = [Serial]	
							FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials]
							WHERE [DocItemSerialID] = @SerialId
							SET @RootId = 0
							SET @SerialId = NULL
						/**/
						END
						ELSE IF(ISNULL(@OprType,0) = 2 OR  ISNULL(@OprType,0) = 3)/*Draft Or Movement*/
						BEGIN
						/**/
							/*----------------*/
							SELECT TOP 1 
								 @Serial = [Serial]							
								,@RootId = [RootId]
							FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials]
							WHERE [DocItemSerialID] = @SerialId
							SET @RootId = ISNULL(@RootId,0)
							IF(@RootId = 0)
							BEGIN
								SET @RootId = @SerialId
							END
							/*----------------*/
						/**/
						END						 			
												
						INSERT INTO dbo.WSDocItemSerials (
							ItemId,
							SerialId,
							[Description],
							WHId,
							UserId,	
							Month1,	
							FPId, 
							RegisterDate, 
							RegisterDatePersian, 
							AppType,
							LanguageID,
							FirstRegisterDatePersian,
							FirstRegisterUserId,
							FirstRegisterDate,
							[Serial],
							[RootId])	
						VALUES( 
							@DocItemId,
							@SerialId,
							@ordItemSerialDescription,
							/*@WhID,*/
							ISNULL(@WareHouseId,@WhID),
							@UserId, 
							@Month1, 
							@FPId, 
							GETDATE(), 
							@RegisterDatePersian, 
							@AppType,
							@LanguageID,
							@RegisterDatePersian,
							@UserId,
							GETDATE(),
							@Serial,
							@RootId)
		
						SET @docItemSerialId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
						/*Insert records in lang table*/			
						INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials_Lang]
						(
							[DocItemSerialID]
						   ,[LanguageID]
						   ,[Description]
						)
						SELECT
							@docItemSerialId,
							OISLN.LanguageID,
							OISLN.[Description]
						FROM dbo.WSOrderItemSerials_Lang OISLN 
						WHERE  OISLN.OrderItemSerialID = @OrderItemSerialID
		
						FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Serial INTO  @SerialId, @ordItemSerialDescription, @OrderItemSerialID
					/*---------------------------------------*/
					END
					CLOSE Crsr_Add_Serial
					DEALLOCATE Crsr_Add_Serial
					/*----------------- End user cursor for insert serial in doc --------------------------------*/				
					/*------------------- tran3 -----------------------*/
					IF (@@ERROR > 0)
					BEGIN 
						SET @ErrorFrom='Insert Into DocItemSerial' 
						SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR 
						CLOSE Crsr_Add_Serial
						DEALLOCATE Crsr_Add_Serial
					ROLLBACK TRAN 
					END		
					ELSE 
					COMMIT TRAN 
					/*------------------- tran3 -----------------------*/
				FETCH NEXT FROM crsr INTO @OrderItemID, @ordIPrdId, @ordIQuantity, 
										  @ordIDescription,@ordIFCR, @ordIFCRI,
										  @ordITracing
										  ,@ordIPrdUnitId
										  ,@ordIQuantityTotal
										  ,@ordIPrdUnitIdI,@ordIPrdUnitIRateResult
										  ,@ordIPrdNickname
			/*---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
			END
			/*--*/
			CLOSE crsr
			DEALLOCATE crsr
			/*--*/
			/*------------------- tran2 ----------------------*/
			IF (@@ERROR > 0)
			BEGIN 
				SET @ErrorFrom='Insert Into DocItems' 
				SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR 
			ROLLBACK TRAN 
			END		
			ELSE 
			COMMIT TRAN 
			/*------------------- tran2 ----------------------*/			
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Wrhs INTO @WareHouseId
	END
	CLOSE Crsr_Wrhs
	DEALLOCATE Crsr_Wrhs
		
	/*--------------------------------------------*/

/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*--------------------------- MAIN TRANSACTION END --------------------------*/
/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
IF (@ErrorNumber > 0)
begin
	RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)
	ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
	Set @DraftId = 0
	Return @DraftId
end
ELSE
BEGIN
	COMMIT TRANSACTION Main
	Return @DraftId
END	
/*--*/
END







GO
-- [dbo].[MobspIssueDuplicatePreOrder]





/*AI000116*/
/*AI14000302 - Add Fields*/
/*AI14010429 - Add field - Add length nvarchar to max*/
/*AI14010605 - Add actInsertDupPordWithOldPrice and add pordIQuantityTotal*/
/*AI14010614 - Add field*/
/*AI14010706 - Add PrdUnitIdI - Add DefPayTypeId*/
/*AI14020119 - Add PrdNickname*/
/*AI14020219 - Add pordDeadlineDate - Add fields to payment*/
/*AI14020325 - Add new fields*/
/*AI14020412 - Set deactive payment for old preorder*/
/*AI14020428 - Add pordILinkAccWHIdStr*/
/*AI14020709 - Add field - Change PrdAutoBill - Add pordpayAccountNumber*/
/*RZ14030129-Add ordpayDocDateTemp*/
/*RZ14030301- Add pordCurrencyUnitIdI and amounts*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspIssueDuplicatePreOrder]
/*
	DECLARE @InsertedPreOrderId INT = 0,
	@InsertedPordNumber BIGINT = 0,
	@InsertedPordNumberRevision BIGINT = 0,
	@ErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) =''
	EXEC MobspIssueDuplicatePreOrder		 
	@PreOrderId = 2481,
	@UserId = 3,
	@Month1 = 8,
	@RegisterDate = '2020-11-14',
	@RegisterDatePersian = '13990824',
	@AppType = 'dasti',
	/**/
	@InsertedPreOrderId = @InsertedPreOrderId OUTPUT,
	@InsertedPordNumber = @InsertedPordNumber OUTPUT,
	@InsertedPordNumberRevision = @InsertedPordNumberRevision OUTPUT,
	@ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage OUTPUT	
	
	SELECT @InsertedPreOrderId,@InsertedPordNumber,
	@InsertedPordNumberRevision	, @ErrorMessage						
*/
(
@PreOrderId INT,
@UserId INT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@AppType VARCHAR(20),
/**/
@InsertedPreOrderId INT OUTPUT,
@InsertedPordNumber BIGINT OUTPUT,
@InsertedPordNumberRevision BIGINT OUTPUT,
@ErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
DECLARE @ErrorNumber INT
SET @ErrorMessage = ''
DECLARE @RegisterTime NVARCHAR(8),@RegisterDay NVARCHAR(10)
SET @RegisterTime = CAST(@RegisterDate AS TIME)
SET @RegisterDay = DATEPART(dw,@RegisterDate)
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** TRANSACTION *************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
/**/	
BEGIN TRY  
    		
	/*======================= Get pordNumberRevison ==========================*/			
	DECLARE @pordNumberRevision BIGINT,@FPId INT,@pordNumber BIGINT
	/*SET @FPId = (SELECT TOP 1 [FPId] FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder] WHERE PreOrderID = @PreOrderId)*/
	SELECT TOP 1 @FPId = [FPId] , @pordNumber = [pordNumber] FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder] WHERE PreOrderID = @PreOrderId
	SELECT @pordNumberRevision =  dbo.WSfnReturnPreOrderNumberRevision(@PreOrderId, @FPId)
	SET @InsertedPordNumberRevision = @pordNumberRevision		
	SET @InsertedPordNumber = @pordNumber		
	/*=============================== PreOrder ==========================*/	
	/*-------------------- TRAN 1 --------------------------*/
	DECLARE @InsertedPordId INT
	BEGIN TRAN	
		DECLARE @pordNumRef NVARCHAR(150)
		IF EXISTS (
		SELECT * FROM dbo.CrmsysRollPermission
		WHERE FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmPreOrderDataEntry' )
		AND FormActionId = (SELECT Id FROM dbo.CrmsysFormActions WHERE ActionName = N'actAutoCalcNumRef'
								AND FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmPreOrderDataEntry' ))
		AND RollId = (SELECT TOP 1 RollId FROM dbo.UsersPermission WHERE UserId = @UserId AND PrjId = 11/*CRM*/))
		BEGIN
		/**/	
			DECLARE @pordCompanyId INT,@pordProjectId INT, @pordReferUserId INT,@LanguageID INT,
			@PreOrderOprID INT,@pordTypeId INT
			SET @LanguageID = (SELECT TOP 1 [DefaultLanguageID] FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting])
			DECLARE @TmpTable TABLE ([pordnumRef] NVARCHAR(150))
			SELECT TOP 1
				 @pordCompanyId = [pordCompanyId]			
				,@pordProjectId = [pordProjectId]
				,@pordReferUserId = [pordReferUserId]				
				,@PreOrderOprID = [PreOrderOprID]
				,@pordTypeId = [pordTypeId]			
			FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder]
			WHERE PreOrderID = @PreOrderId							
			INSERT INTO @TmpTable([pordnumRef])	
			EXEC dbo.MobspReturnPreOrderNumRef 
			@PordCompanyId = @pordCompanyId,
			@PordProjectId = @pordProjectId,
			@PordReferUserId = @pordReferUserId,
			@PordNumber = @pordNumber,
			@PordNumberRevision = @pordNumberRevision,
			@PreOrderOprId = @PreOrderOprID,
			@PordTypeId = @pordTypeId,
			@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
			@DefaultLanguageID = @LanguageID,
			@selectedNumRef = @pordNumRef OUTPUT
		/**/
		END	
		INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrder]
			([pordTitle]
			,[pordNumber]
			,[pordDate]
			,[pordCompanyId]
			,[pordCompanyPersonId]
			,[pordProjectId]
			,[pordSubject]
			,[pordItemCount]
			,[pordItemsAmount]
			,[pordInstallAmount]

				,@pordITaxSumCurrencyI

				)

				([PreOrderItemID]
				,[LanguageID]

				,[pordIDiscountDesc]					
				/*AI14020325 - End*/
				/*AI14020709- Add fields*/
				,[pordIDescriptionI]		
				/*AI14020709- End*/
				)
			SELECT 
				 @InsertedPordItemId
				,[LanguageID]
				,[pordIDescription]
				,[pordIQuantityDesc]
				,[pordIPrdNickname] /*AI14020119- Add PrdNickname*/
				/*AI14020325 - Add new fields*/
				,[pordIDiscountDesc]					
				/*AI14020325 - End*/
				/*AI14020709- Add fields*/
				,[pordIDescriptionI]		
				/*AI14020709- End*/
			FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems_Lang]
			WHERE [PreOrderItemID] = @PreOrderItemID

			/*=========================== PreOrderItemSpecification=======================*/
			DECLARE @PreOrderItemSpcflID INT,@Specification NVARCHAR(300),@ISFPId INT,
			@SpecificationI NVARCHAR(300),@SpecificationII NVARCHAR(300),@ISLanguageID INT
			/*--------------------------------------------*/
			DECLARE Crsr_Add_Item_Spec CURSOR LOCAL FOR
				SELECT
					 [PreOrderItemSpcflID]
					,[Specification]
					,[FPId]
					,[SpecificationI]
					,[SpecificationII]
					,[LanguageID]					
				FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItemSpecification]
				WHERE [PreOrderItemId] = @PreOrderItemID
			OPEN Crsr_Add_Item_Spec
			FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Item_Spec INTO  @PreOrderItemSpcflID,@Specification,@ISFPId,
													 @SpecificationI,@SpecificationII,@ISLanguageID
			WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
			BEGIN
				/*--------------------------------------------*/
				DECLARE @PordItemSpcflId INT
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrderItemSpecification]
					([PreOrderItemId]
					,[Specification]
					,[UserId]
					,[Month1]
					,[FPId]
					,[RegisterDate]
					,[RegisterDatePersian]
					,[SpecificationI]
					,[SpecificationII]
					,[AppType]
					,[LanguageID]
					,[FirstRegisterUserId]
					,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
					,[FirstRegisterDate])
				VALUES
					(@InsertedPordItemId
					,@Specification
					,@UserId
					,@Month1
					,@ISFPId
					,@RegisterDate
					,@RegisterDatePersian					
					,@SpecificationI
					,@SpecificationII
					,@AppType
					,@ISLanguageID
					,@UserId
					,@RegisterDatePersian
					,@RegisterDate)
				SET @PordItemSpcflId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()

					,[SpecificationII]
				FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItemSpecification_Lang]

				/*--------------------------------------------*/
				FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Item_Spec INTO @PreOrderItemSpcflID,@Specification,@ISFPId,
														@SpecificationI,@SpecificationII,@ISLanguageID 
			END
			CLOSE Crsr_Add_Item_Spec
			DEALLOCATE Crsr_Add_Item_Spec
		
			/*--------------------------------------------*/
			/*============================================================================*/
			/*--------------------------------------------*/
			FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Item INTO @PreOrderItemID,@pordIPrdId,@pordIQuantity,@pordIPrice,@pordITotalPrice,@pordIDescription,	
										   @IFPId,@pordIQuantityDesc,@pordIDiscount,@pordIDiscountI,@pordIDiscountPercent,@pordIDiscountIPercent,
										   @pordIPrdUnitId,@pordICurrencyUnitId,@pordIOrderView,
										   @pordIFCR,@pordITax,@pordITaxIndex,@pordICalculateTax,@pordINotVisible,@pordIFCRI,
										   @pordINotInSum,@pordIWHId,@pordIRound,@ILanguageID,
										   @pordIRoundPlus,@pordIPrdPriceGroupID,@pordITracing,@pordIPriceSum,
										   @pordIDiscountSum,@pordIDiscountISum,@pordITaxSum,
										   @pordIDeliveryTermId,@pordIDeliveryDate,@pordIDeliveryDatePersian
										   ,@pordIQuantityTotal /*AI14010605- Add actInsertDupPordWithOldPrice and add pordIQuantityTotal*/
										   ,@pordIPrdColorId /*AI14010614- Add prdColorId*/
										   ,@pordIPrdUnitIdI,@pordIPrdUnitIRateResult/*AI14010706- Add PrdUnitIdI*/
										   ,@pordIPrdNickname /*AI14020119- Add PrdNickname*/
										   /*AI14020325 - Add new fields*/
										   ,@pordIDiscountDesc,@pordIDeliveryTime,@pordIDeliveryDay,@pordIDeliveryEndDate
										   ,@pordIDeliveryEndDatePersian,@pordIDeliveryEndTime,@pordIDeliveryEndDay		
										   /*AI14020325 - End*/
										   ,@pordILinkAccWHIdStr/*AI14020428 - Add pordILinkAccWHIdStr*/
										   /*AI14020709- Add fields*/
											,@pordIValidityExpireDate,@pordIValidityExpireDatePersian
										   ,@pordIPrdAutoCalcRate,@pordIPrdAutoCalcInflationRateInfo/*AI14020709- Change PrdAutoBill*/										  				
										   ,@pordIDescriptionI			
										   /*AI14020709- End*/
										   /*RZ14030301- Add pordCurrencyUnitIdI and amounts*/
										   ,@pordICurrencyUnitIdI,@pordICurrencyUnitIRate,@pordIPriceCurrencyI
										   ,@pordITotalPriceCurrencyI,@pordIDiscountCurrencyI,@pordIDiscountICurrencyI 
										   ,@pordITaxCurrencyI,@pordIRoundCurrencyI,@pordIRoundPlusCurrencyI
										   ,@pordIPriceSumCurrencyI,@pordIDiscountSumCurrencyI,@pordIDiscountISumCurrencyI,@pordITaxSumCurrencyI			
										   /*RZ14030301- End*/
		END
		CLOSE Crsr_Add_Item
		DEALLOCATE Crsr_Add_Item
		
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
	IF (@@ERROR > 0)
	BEGIN SET @ErrorMessage +=',Error In Insert PreOrderItems' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
	ELSE 
	COMMIT TRAN 	

	/*=========================== PreOrderPayments ====================*/	
	/*-------------------- TRAN 3 --------------------------*/
	DECLARE @PreOrderPaymentId INT,@ImportDate NVARCHAR(8),@pordpayTypeId INT,
	@pordpayDocId INT,@pordpayDocTypeId INT,@pordpayAmount NUMERIC(18,2),
	@pordpayDocNumber NVARCHAR(50),@pordpayDocDate NVARCHAR(8),
	@pordpayDocAmount NUMERIC(18,2),@pordpayDescription NVARCHAR(2000),@PFPId INT,
	@ImportDateTime DATETIME,@pordpayDocDateTime DATETIME,@PLanguageID INT,
	/*AI14020219- Add fields to payment*/
	@pordpayDeActive BIT,@pordpayAmountTemp NUMERIC(18,2)
	/*AI14020219- End*/
	,@pordpayAccountNumber NVARCHAR(50) = NULL/*AI14020709- Add pordpayAccountNumber*/
	/*RZ14030129- Add ordpayDocDateTemp*/
	,@pordpayDocDateTemp NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,@pordpayDocDateTimeTemp DATETIME = NULL
	/*RZ14030129- End*/
	BEGIN TRAN						
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		DECLARE Crsr_Add_Pay CURSOR LOCAL FOR				
			SELECT 
				 [PreOrderPaymentId]
				,[ImportDate]
				,[pordpayTypeId]
				,[pordpayDocId]
				,[pordpayDocTypeId]
				,[pordpayAmount]
				,[pordpayDocNumber]
				,[pordpayDocDate]
				,[pordpayDocAmount]
				,[pordpayDescription]
				,[FPId]
				,[ImportDateTime]
				,[pordpayDocDateTime]
				,[LanguageID]
				/*AI14020219- Add fields to payment*/
				,[pordpayDeActive]
				,[pordpayAmountTemp]				
				/*AI14020219- End*/
				,[pordpayAccountNumber]/*AI14020709- Add pordpayAccountNumber*/
				/*RZ14030129- Add ordpayDocDateTemp*/
				,[pordpayDocDateTemp]
				,[pordpayDocDateTimeTemp]
				/*RZ14030129- End*/
			FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderPayments]	
			WHERE [pordpayPreOrderId] = @PreOrderId
		OPEN Crsr_Add_Pay
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Pay INTO @PreOrderPaymentId,@ImportDate,@pordpayTypeId,
										  @pordpayDocId,@pordpayDocTypeId,@pordpayAmount,
										  @pordpayDocNumber,@pordpayDocDate,
										  @pordpayDocAmount,@pordpayDescription,@PFPId,
										  @ImportDateTime,@pordpayDocDateTime,@PLanguageID,
										  /*AI14020219- Add fields to payment*/
										  @pordpayDeActive,@pordpayAmountTemp
										  /*AI14020219- End*/
										  ,@pordpayAccountNumber/*AI14020709- Add pordpayAccountNumber*/
										  /*RZ14030129- Add ordpayDocDateTemp*/
										  ,@pordpayDocDateTemp,@pordpayDocDateTimeTemp 
										  /*RZ14030129- End*/
		WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
		BEGIN
			/*--------------------------------------------*/
			DECLARE @InsertedPordPayId INT
			
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrderPayments]
				([ImportDate]
				,[pordpayTypeId]
				,[pordpayPreOrderId]
				,[pordpayDocId]
				,[pordpayDocTypeId]
				,[pordpayAmount]
				,[pordpayDocNumber]
				,[pordpayDocDate]
				,[pordpayDocAmount]
				,[pordpayDescription]
				,[pordpayFirstRegisterUserId]
				,[pordpayFirstRegisteDatePersian]
				,[pordpayFirstRegisteDate]
				,[UserId]
				,[FPId]
				,[Month1]
				,[RegisterDate]
				,[RegisterDatePersian]
				,[ImportDateTime]
				,[pordpayDocDateTime]				
				,[AppType]
				,[LanguageID]
				,[FirstRegisterUserId]
				,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
				,[FirstRegisterDate]
				/*AI14020219- Add fields to payment*/
				,[pordpayDeActive]
				,[pordpayAmountTemp]				
				/*AI14020219- End*/
				,[pordpayAccountNumber]/*AI14020709- Add pordpayAccountNumber*/
				/*RZ14030129- Add ordpayDocDateTemp*/
				,[pordpayDocDateTemp]
				,[pordpayDocDateTimeTemp]
				/*RZ14030129- End*/
				)
			VALUES
				 (@ImportDate
				,@pordpayTypeId
				,@InsertedPordId
				,@pordpayDocId
				,@pordpayDocTypeId
				,@pordpayAmount
				,@pordpayDocNumber
				,@pordpayDocDate
				,@pordpayDocAmount
				,@pordpayDescription
				,@UserId
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@RegisterDate
				,@UserId
				,@PFPId
				,@Month1
				,@RegisterDate
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@ImportDateTime
				,@pordpayDocDateTime
				,@AppType
				,@PLanguageID
				,@UserId
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@RegisterDate
				/*AI14020219- Add fields to payment*/
				,@pordpayDeActive
				,@pordpayAmountTemp				
				/*AI14020219- End*/
				,@pordpayAccountNumber/*AI14020709- Add pordpayAccountNumber*/
				/*RZ14030129- Add ordpayDocDateTemp*/
				,@pordpayDocDateTemp
				,@pordpayDocDateTimeTemp
				/*RZ14030129- End*/
				)
			
			SET @InsertedPordPayId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
			/*Insert records in lang table*/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrderPayments_Lang]
				([PreOrderPaymentId]
				,[LanguageID]
				,[pordpayDescription])
			SELECT
				 @InsertedPordPayId
				,[LanguageID]
				,[pordpayDescription]
			FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderPayments_Lang]
			WHERE PreOrderPaymentId = @PreOrderPaymentId
			/*--------------------------------------------*/
			/*AI14020412- Set deactive payment for old preorder*/
			UPDATE [dbo].[WSPreOrderPayments]
			SET pordpayDeActive = 1
			WHERE PreOrderPaymentId = @PreOrderPaymentId
			/*AI14020412- End*/
			/*--------------------------------------------*/
			FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Pay INTO @PreOrderPaymentId,@ImportDate,@pordpayTypeId,
											  @pordpayDocId,@pordpayDocTypeId,@pordpayAmount,
											  @pordpayDocNumber,@pordpayDocDate,
											  @pordpayDocAmount,@pordpayDescription,@PFPId,
											  @ImportDateTime,@pordpayDocDateTime,@PLanguageID,
											  /*AI14020219- Add fields to payment*/
											  @pordpayDeActive,@pordpayAmountTemp
											  /*AI14020219- End*/
											  ,@pordpayAccountNumber/*AI14020709- Add pordpayAccountNumber*/
											  /*RZ14030129- Add ordpayDocDateTemp*/
											  ,@pordpayDocDateTemp,@pordpayDocDateTimeTemp 
											  /*RZ14030129- End*/
		END
		CLOSE Crsr_Add_Pay
		DEALLOCATE Crsr_Add_Pay
		
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
	IF (@@ERROR > 0)
	BEGIN SET @ErrorMessage +=',Error In Insert PreOrderPayments' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
	ELSE 
	COMMIT TRAN 	
	/*=============== PreOrderPrdGroupMustExistReasons ====================*/	
	/*-------------------- TRAN 4 --------------------------*/
	DECLARE @PrdGroupMustExistReasonId INT,@PrdGroupMustExistReasonGroupId INT,
	@CompanyId INT,@cmpProjectId INT,@PrdFirstGroupID INT,@Reason NVARCHAR(4000),
	@RFPId INT,@RLanguageID INT
	BEGIN TRAN						
		/*--------------------------------------------*/

			SELECT 
			     [PrdGroupMustExistReasonId]
				,[PrdGroupMustExistReasonGroupId]
				,[CompanyId]
				,[cmpProjectId]
				,[PrdFirstGroupID]
				,[Reason]
				,[FPId]
				,[LanguageID]
			FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderPrdGroupMustExistReasons]
			WHERE [PreOrderId] = @PreOrderId
		OPEN Crsr_Add_Reason
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Reason INTO @PrdGroupMustExistReasonId,@PrdGroupMustExistReasonGroupId,
											 @CompanyId,@cmpProjectId,@PrdFirstGroupID,@Reason,
											 @RFPId,@RLanguageID
		WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
		BEGIN
			/*--------------------------------------------*/

			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrderPrdGroupMustExistReasons]
				([PrdGroupMustExistReasonGroupId]
				,[CompanyId]
				,[cmpProjectId]
				,[PreOrderId]
				,[PrdFirstGroupID]
				,[Reason]
				,[FirstRegisterDate]
				,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
				,[FirstRegisterUserId]
				,[UserId]
				,[FPId]
				,[Month1]
				,[RegisterDate]
				,[RegisterDatePersian]				
				,[AppType]
				,[LanguageID])
			VALUES
				(@PrdGroupMustExistReasonGroupId
				,@CompanyId
				,@cmpProjectId
				,@InsertedPordId
				,@PrdFirstGroupID
				,@Reason
				,@RegisterDate
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@UserId
				,@UserId
				,@RFPId
				,@Month1
				,@RegisterDate
				,@RegisterDatePersian				
				,@AppType
				,@RLanguageID)

			SET @InsertedReasonId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
			/*Insert records in lang table*/

				([PrdGroupMustExistReasonId]
