				,[Reason]
			FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderPrdGroupMustExistReasons_Lang]
			WHERE PrdGroupMustExistReasonId = @PrdGroupMustExistReasonId
			/*--------------------------------------------*/
			FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Reason INTO @PrdGroupMustExistReasonId,@PrdGroupMustExistReasonGroupId,
												 @CompanyId,@cmpProjectId,@PrdFirstGroupID,@Reason,
												 @RFPId,@RLanguageID
		END
		CLOSE Crsr_Add_Reason
		DEALLOCATE Crsr_Add_Reason
		
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
	IF (@@ERROR > 0)
	BEGIN SET @ErrorMessage +=',Error In Insert PreOrderPrdGroupMustExistReasons' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
	ELSE 
	COMMIT TRAN 	

	/*=============== CrmFilesDirectory ===========================*/	
	/*-------------------- TRAN 5 --------------------------*/
	DECLARE @FileDirectoryID INT,@FileDirectoryTypeId INT,
	@MasterId INT,@DetailId INT,@SubDetailId INT,@Title NVARCHAR(50),
	@FolderName NVARCHAR(50),@FileName NVARCHAR(50),
	@FilePath NVARCHAR(200),@CopyFromPath NVARCHAR(200),
	@DocDate NVARCHAR(8),@DocDay NVARCHAR(20),
	@DocTime NVARCHAR(15),@DocDateTime DATETIME,
	@TypeOprObjectId INT,@FDLanguageID INT,@FDYear1 SMALLINT,
	@OrderView INT,@TypeOprObjectIdI INT,@TypeOprObjectIdII INT
	

	BEGIN TRAN						
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		DECLARE Crsr_Add_File CURSOR LOCAL FOR				
			SELECT 
				 [FileDirectoryID]
				,[FileDirectoryTypeId]
				,[MasterId]
				,[DetailId]
				,[SubDetailId]
				,[Title]
				,[FolderName]
				,[FileName]
				,[FilePath]
				,[CopyFromPath]				
				,[DocDate]
				,[DocDay]
				,[DocTime]
				,[DocDateTime]
				,[TypeOprObjectId]
				,[LanguageID]
				,[Year1]
				,[OrderView]
				,[TypeOprObjectIdI]
				,[TypeOprObjectIdII]
			FROM [dbo].[CrmFilesDirectory]
			WHERE TypeOprObjectId = @PreOrderId
			AND FileDirectoryTypeId IN (SELECT FileDirectoryTypeID 
										FROM [dbo].[CrmFileDirectoryType]
										WHERE FileDirectoryTypeOprId = 10) /*PreOrder*/
			ORDER BY [OrderView]
		OPEN Crsr_Add_File
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_File INTO @FileDirectoryID,@FileDirectoryTypeId,
										   @MasterId,@DetailId,@SubDetailId,@Title,
										   @FolderName,@FileName,@FilePath,@CopyFromPath,
										   @DocDate,@DocDay,@DocTime,@DocDateTime,
										   @TypeOprObjectId,@FDLanguageID,@FDYear1,
										   @OrderView,@TypeOprObjectIdI,@TypeOprObjectIdII
		WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
		BEGIN
			/*--------------------------------------------*/
			DECLARE @InsertedFileId INT
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmFilesDirectory]
				([FileDirectoryTypeId]
				,[MasterId]
				,[DetailId]
				,[SubDetailId]
				,[Title]
				,[FolderName]
				,[FileName]
				,[FilePath]
				,[CopyFromPath]
				,[UserId]
				,[Year1]
				,[Month1]
				,[RegisterDate]
				,[RegisterDatePersian]
				,[DocDate]
				,[DocDay]
				,[DocTime]				
				,[DocDateTime]
				,[TypeOprObjectId]
				,[AppType]
				,[LanguageID]
				,[FirstRegisterUserId]
				,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
				,[FirstRegisterDate]
				,[OrderView]
				,[TypeOprObjectIdI]
				,[TypeOprObjectIdII])
			VALUES
				(@FileDirectoryTypeId
				,@MasterId
				,@DetailId
				,@SubDetailId
				,@Title
				,@FolderName
				,@FileName
				,@FilePath
				,@CopyFromPath
				,@UserId
				,@FDYear1
				,@Month1
				,@RegisterDate
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@DocDate
				,@DocDay
				,@DocTime			
				,@DocDateTime
				,@InsertedPordId
				,@AppType
				,@FDLanguageID
				,@UserId
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@RegisterDate
				,@OrderView
				,@TypeOprObjectIdI
				,@TypeOprObjectIdII)

			SET @InsertedFileId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
			/*Insert records in lang table*/
			
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmFilesDirectory_Lang]
				([FileDirectoryID]
				,[LanguageID]
				,[Title])
			SELECT
				 @InsertedFileId
				,[LanguageID]
				,[Title]
			FROM [dbo].[CrmFilesDirectory_Lang]
			WHERE FileDirectoryID = @FileDirectoryID
			/*--------------------------------------------*/
			FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_File INTO @FileDirectoryID,@FileDirectoryTypeId,
											   @MasterId,@DetailId,@SubDetailId,@Title,
											   @FolderName,@FileName,@FilePath,@CopyFromPath,
											   @DocDate,@DocDay,@DocTime,@DocDateTime,
											   @TypeOprObjectId,@FDLanguageID,@FDYear1,
											   @OrderView,@TypeOprObjectIdI,@TypeOprObjectIdII
		END
		CLOSE Crsr_Add_File
		DEALLOCATE Crsr_Add_File
		
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
	IF (@@ERROR > 0)
	BEGIN SET @ErrorMessage +=',Error In Insert CrmFilesDirectory' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
	ELSE 
	COMMIT TRAN 	
		
	/*=============== WSPreOrderCommissions =====================*/	
	/*-------------------- TRAN 6 --------------------------*/
	DECLARE @PreOrderCommissionId INT,@CommDateTime DATETIME,
	@CommDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),@CommCmpId INT,
	@CommUserId INT,@CommBaseAmountDec NUMERIC(18,2),
	@CommBaseAmountDecDesc NVARCHAR(4000),@CommBaseAmount NUMERIC(18,2),
	@CommRate FLOAT,@CommRateDesc NVARCHAR(4000),@CommAmount NUMERIC(18,2),
	@CommAmountCurrUnId INT,@CommAmountCurrencyRate FLOAT,
	@CommEffectiveDateTime DATETIME,@CommEffectiveDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
	@CommPayTypeId INT,@CommStatusId INT,@CommDesc NVARCHAR(4000),
	@CommIsVerified BIT,@CommVerifierUserId INT,@CommVerifiedDateTime DATETIME,
	@CommVerifiedDatePersian VARCHAR(8),@CommIsPaid BIT,@CommIsPaidUserId INT,
	@CommIsPaidDateTime DATETIME,@CommIsPaidDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
	@CommIsPaidPayRollUserCycleId INT,@CoYear1 SMALLINT,@CoLanguageID INT

	BEGIN TRAN						
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		DECLARE Crsr_Add_Comm CURSOR LOCAL FOR	
			SELECT 
				 [PreOrderCommissionId]
				,[CommDateTime]
				,[CommDatePersian]				
				,[CommCmpId]
				,[CommUserId]
				,[CommBaseAmountDec]
				,[CommBaseAmountDecDesc]
				,[CommBaseAmount]
				,[CommRate]
				,[CommRateDesc]
				,[CommAmount]
				,[CommAmountCurrUnId]
				,[CommAmountCurrencyRate]
				,[CommEffectiveDateTime]
				,[CommEffectiveDatePersian]
				,[CommPayTypeId]
				,[CommStatusId]
				,[CommDesc]
				,[CommIsVerified]
				,[CommVerifierUserId]
				,[CommVerifiedDateTime]
				,[CommVerifiedDatePersian]
				,[CommIsPaid]
				,[CommIsPaidUserId]
				,[CommIsPaidDateTime]
				,[CommIsPaidDatePersian]
				,[CommIsPaidPayRollUserCycleId]				
				,[Year1]				
				,[LanguageID]
			FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderCommissions]
			WHERE [CommPreOrderId] = @PreOrderId		
		OPEN Crsr_Add_Comm
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Comm INTO @PreOrderCommissionId,@CommDateTime,@CommDatePersian,@CommCmpId,
										   @CommUserId,@CommBaseAmountDec,@CommBaseAmountDecDesc,@CommBaseAmount,
										   @CommRate,@CommRateDesc,@CommAmount,@CommAmountCurrUnId,@CommAmountCurrencyRate,
										   @CommEffectiveDateTime,@CommEffectiveDatePersian,
										   @CommPayTypeId,@CommStatusId,@CommDesc,@CommIsVerified,@CommVerifierUserId,@CommVerifiedDateTime,
										   @CommVerifiedDatePersian,@CommIsPaid,@CommIsPaidUserId,
										   @CommIsPaidDateTime,@CommIsPaidDatePersian,
										   @CommIsPaidPayRollUserCycleId,@CoYear1,@CoLanguageID
		WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
		BEGIN
			/*--------------------------------------------*/
			DECLARE @InsertedCommId INT
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrderCommissions]
				([CommDateTime]
				,[CommDatePersian]
				,[CommPreOrderId]
				,[CommCmpId]
				,[CommUserId]
				,[CommBaseAmountDec]
				,[CommBaseAmountDecDesc]
				,[CommBaseAmount]
				,[CommRate]
				,[CommRateDesc]
				,[CommAmount]
				,[CommAmountCurrUnId]
				,[CommAmountCurrencyRate]
				,[CommEffectiveDateTime]
				,[CommEffectiveDatePersian]
				,[CommPayTypeId]
				,[CommStatusId]
				,[CommDesc]
				/*,[CommIsVerified]
				,[CommVerifierUserId]
				,[CommVerifiedDateTime]
				,[CommVerifiedDatePersian]*/
				,[CommIsPaid]
				,[CommIsPaidUserId]
				,[CommIsPaidDateTime]
				,[CommIsPaidDatePersian]
				,[CommIsPaidPayRollUserCycleId]
				,[FirstRegisterUserId]
				,[FirstRegisterDate]
				,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
				,[UserId]
				,[Year1]
				,[Month1]
				,[RegisterDate]
				,[RegisterDatePersian]
				,[AppType]
				,[LanguageID])
			VALUES
				(@CommDateTime
				,@CommDatePersian
				,@InsertedPordId
				,@CommCmpId
				,@CommUserId
				,@CommBaseAmountDec
				,@CommBaseAmountDecDesc
				,@CommBaseAmount
				,@CommRate
				,@CommRateDesc
				,@CommAmount
				,@CommAmountCurrUnId
				,@CommAmountCurrencyRate
				,@CommEffectiveDateTime
				,@CommEffectiveDatePersian
				,@CommPayTypeId
				,@CommStatusId
				,@CommDesc
				/*,@CommIsVerified
				,@CommVerifierUserId
				,@CommVerifiedDateTime
				,@CommVerifiedDatePersian*/
				,@CommIsPaid
				,@CommIsPaidUserId
				,@CommIsPaidDateTime
				,@CommIsPaidDatePersian
				,@CommIsPaidPayRollUserCycleId
				,@UserId
				,@RegisterDate
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@UserId
				,@CoYear1
				,@Month1
				,@RegisterDate
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@AppType
				,@CoLanguageID)

			SET @InsertedCommId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
			/*Insert records in lang table*/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrderCommissions_Lang]
				([PreOrderCommissionId]
				,[LanguageID]
				,[CommBaseAmountDecDesc]
				,[CommRateDesc]
				,[CommDesc])
			SELECT
				 @InsertedCommId
				,[LanguageID]
				,[CommBaseAmountDecDesc]
				,[CommRateDesc]
				,[CommDesc]
			FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderCommissions_Lang]
			WHERE [PreOrderCommissionId] = @PreOrderCommissionId

			/*--------------------------------------------*/
			FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Comm INTO @PreOrderCommissionId,@CommDateTime,@CommDatePersian,@CommCmpId,
											   @CommUserId,@CommBaseAmountDec,@CommBaseAmountDecDesc,@CommBaseAmount,
										       @CommRate,@CommRateDesc,@CommAmount,@CommAmountCurrUnId,@CommAmountCurrencyRate,
										       @CommEffectiveDateTime,@CommEffectiveDatePersian,
										       @CommPayTypeId,@CommStatusId,@CommDesc,@CommIsVerified,@CommVerifierUserId,@CommVerifiedDateTime,
										       @CommVerifiedDatePersian,@CommIsPaid,@CommIsPaidUserId,
										       @CommIsPaidDateTime,@CommIsPaidDatePersian,
										       @CommIsPaidPayRollUserCycleId,@CoYear1,@CoLanguageID
		END
		CLOSE Crsr_Add_Comm
		DEALLOCATE Crsr_Add_Comm
		
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
	


	/*--------------------------------------------*/
	IF (@@ERROR > 0)
	BEGIN SET @ErrorMessage +=',Error In Insert WSPreOrderCommissions' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
	ELSE 
	COMMIT TRAN 

	/*=========================================================================*/	
	/*--------------------------------------------*/
	/*AI14010605- Add actInsertDupPordWithOldPrice and add pordIQuantityTotal*/
	IF(ISNULL(@HavePermissionInsertDupPordWithOldPrice,0) = 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		/*------------------------------------------------*/
		DECLARE @LangId INT
		SET @LangId = (SELECT TOP 1 [DefaultLanguageID] FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting])
		IF(ISNULL(@LangId,0) = 0) 
			SET @LangId = 1065
		EXEC dbo.MobspCalcAndUpdatePreOrderAmounts @InsertedPordId,@Month1,@LangId,@LangId	
		/*------------------------------------------------*/
	/**/
	END
	/*AI14010605- End*/
	/*--------------------------------------------*/
	/*=========================================================================*/		
	IF(ISNULL(@ErrorMessage,'') = '')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
		SET @InsertedPreOrderId = @InsertedPordId
		SET @InsertedPordNumber = @pordNumber
		SET @InsertedPordNumberRevision = @pordNumberRevision
		SELECT 1					
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(ISNULL(@ErrorMessage,'') <> '')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
		SET @InsertedPreOrderId = 0
		SET @InsertedPordNumber = 0
		SET @InsertedPordNumberRevision = 0
		 
		SELECT 0			
	/**/
	END	
END TRY  
BEGIN CATCH  
	/*RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)*/
	ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
    SET @InsertedPreOrderId = 0
	SET @InsertedPordNumber = 0
	SET @InsertedPordNumberRevision = 0
	
	SELECT 0	 
END CATCH  	
/********************************************************************************/
/**/
END







GO

/*RZ14030119- Modify ordTime*/
/*RZ14030305- Add ordCurrencyUnitIdI and amounts*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspIssueOrderFromDoc]	
(		
/* 
	EXEC MobspIssueOrderFromDoc 
*/
@DocId INT,
@OrderDate NVARCHAR(8),
@OrderDateTime DATETIME,
@OrderOprId INT,
@OrderReferUserId INT,
@OrderPrintFormatID INT,
@OrderDefaultSettingId INT= NULL,
@OrderSettlementTypeId INT= NULL,
@OrderNote NVARCHAR(4000)= NULL, 
@OrderNoteLang NVARCHAR(MAX)= NULL,
@OrderProjectId INT,
/**/
@DocItemList NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
/**/
@UserId INT,
@Month1 INT,
@FPId INT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@AppType NVARCHAR(20), 
@DefaultLanguageID INT,
/**/
@InsertedOrderID INT OUTPUT,
@InsertedOrderNumber BIGINT OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
/*----------------- Define Variables ----------------*/
DECLARE @ordTitle NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@ordNumber BIGINT = NULL,@ordDate NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
@ordCompanyId INT = NULL,@ordCompanyPersonId INT = NULL,
@ordSubject NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@ordIsFixed BIT = NULL ,@ordGuarantyId INT = NULL,
@ordSpecialWord NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@ordDescriptionI NVARCHAR(4000) = NULL,@ordDescriptionII NVARCHAR(4000) = NULL,
@ordRLogoId INT = NULL,@ordLLogoId INT = NULL,@ordSigningI NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@ordSigningII NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,@ordSigningIII NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@ordAccountNumberI NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,@ordAccountShabaNumberI NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@ordAccountNumberII NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,@ordAccountShabaNumberII NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@ordTaxIndex NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@ordCalculateTax BIT = NULL,
@ordDay NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,@ordTime NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@ordNote NVARCHAR(4000) = NULL,@ordDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
@ordSerial NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,@ordVerifierRequestId INT = NULL,
@ordVerificationWorkFlowId INT = NULL,@ordCurrencyUnitId INT = NULL,
@ordDeliveryTermId INT = NULL,@ordDefWHId INT = NULL,
@ordDefPrdPriceGroupId INT = NULL, @ordSellerId INT = NULL,
@ordEmailUsersInfoId INT = NULL,@ordEmailUserDefaultSettingId INT = NULL,
@ordSaleCenterId INT = NULL,@ordDefPayTypeId INT = NULL,@ordSignatureId INT = NULL,@ordSettlementTypeId INT = NULL,
@InsertedOldOrderID INT = NULL,@BuyOrderId INT = NULL,
@PreOrderId INT = NULL,@PreOrderVerifierId INT = NULL

/*RZ14020419- Add ordTspSettingId*/
DECLARE @ordTspSettingId INT 
SET @ordTspSettingId = (SELECT TOP(1) TspSettingId FROM [dbo].[TspSetting])
/*RZ14020419- End*/
/*RZ14021203- Add OrderItemId to WSDocItems and add TaxIndexItem*/
DECLARE @ordITaxIndex NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@ordICalculateTax BIT = NULL,
@ordITax  NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@ordITaxSum  NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL
/*RZ14021203- End*/
/*RZ14030305- Add ordCurrencyUnitIdI and amounts*/
,@ordCurrencyUnitIdI INT = NULL,@ordCurrencyUnitIRate FLOAT = NULL
/*RZ14030305- End*/
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
/*---------------------------------------------------*/

SET @ordDate = @OrderDate
SET @ordDateTime = @OrderDateTime
SET @ordTime = CAST(CAST(@ordDateTime AS TIME) AS NVARCHAR(8))/*RZ14030119- Modify ordTime*/
SET @ordDay = DATEPART(dw,@ordDateTime)
SEt @ordIsFixed = 1

SELECT TOP (1)	
	 @ordTitle = [Title]
	,@ordSubject = [Subject]
	,@ordGuarantyId = [GuarantyId]
	,@ordTaxIndex = [TaxIndex]
	,@ordCalculateTax = [CalculateTax]
	,@ordSpecialWord = [SpecialWord]
	,@ordDescriptionI = [DescriptionI]
	,@ordDescriptionII = [DescriptionII]	
	,@ordRLogoId = [RightLogoId]
	,@ordLLogoId = [LeftLogoId]
	,@ordSignatureId = [SignatureId]	
	,@ordSigningI = [SigningI]
	,@ordSigningII = [SigningII]
	,@ordSigningIII = [SigningIII]
	,@ordAccountNumberI = [AccountNumberI]
	,@ordAccountShabaNumberI = [AccountShabaNumberI]
	,@ordAccountNumberII = [AccountNumberII]
	,@ordAccountShabaNumberII = [AccountShabaNumberII]	
	,@ordNote = ISNULL(@OrderNote,[Note])
	,@ordCurrencyUnitId = [CurrencyUnitId]
	,@ordDeliveryTermId = [DeliveryTermId]
	,@ordVerifierRequestId = [VerifierRequestId]
	,@ordDefWHId = [DefWHId]
	,@ordDefPrdPriceGroupId = [DefPrdPriceGroupId]
	,@ordSellerId = [SellerId]
	,@ordEmailUsersInfoId = [EmailUsersInfoId]
	,@ordEmailUserDefaultSettingId = [EmailUserDefaultSettingId]
	,@ordVerificationWorkFlowId = [VerificationWorkFlowId]
	,@ordSaleCenterId = [SaleCenterId]
	,@ordDefPayTypeId = [DefPayTypeId]
	,@ordSettlementTypeId = ISNULL(@OrderSettlementTypeId,SettlementTypeId)	
	/*RZ14021203- Add OrderItemId to WSDocItems and add TaxIndexItem*/
	,@ordITaxIndex = [TaxIndexItem]
    ,@ordICalculateTax = [CalculateTaxItem]
	/*RZ14021203- End*/
	,@ordCurrencyUnitIdI = [CurrencyUnitIdI]/*RZ14030305- Add ordCurrencyUnitIdI and amounts*/
FROM [dbo].[WSOrderDefaultSetting]
WHERE [OrderSettingID] =@OrderDefaultSettingId



/*---------------------------------------------------*/
SELECT TOP(1)
	@ordCompanyId = [CompanyId],
	@ordCompanyPersonId = [CompanyPersonId],
	@InsertedOldOrderID = [OrderId],
	@BuyOrderId = [BuyOrderId],
	@PreOrderId = [PreOrderId],
	@PreOrderVerifierId = (SELECT TOP(1) pordVerifierId FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder] PO WHERE PO.[PreOrderId] = D.[PreOrderId])
FROM [dbo].[WSDoc] D
WHERE DocID = @DocId
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
SELECT @ordNumber =  dbo.WSfnReturnOrderNumber(@FPId, @OrderOprId)
SET @InsertedOrderNumber = @ordNumber

/*RZ14011109-Add ordDailyCounter*/
DECLARE @ordDailyCounter BIGINT
SET @ordDailyCounter = (SELECT [dbo].[WSfnReturnOrderDailyCounter](@OrderOprId, @ordDateTime))
/*RZ14011109-End*/
/*RZ14020324- Add ordDefaultSettingId*/
DECLARE @ordDefaultSettingId INT = NULL
IF(ISNULL(@OrderDefaultSettingId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
/**/
	SET @ordDefaultSettingId = @OrderDefaultSettingId
/**/
END
/*RZ14020324- End*/
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
IF(ISNULL(@ordCurrencyUnitId,0) = 0)
BEGIN
/**/
	SET @ordCurrencyUnitId = (SELECT TOP 1 [CurrencyUnitID]            
								  FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnits]
								  WHERE (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST([CurrencyUnit] AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
										NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) 
										= (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(N'����' AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
										NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) 
								  )
/**/
END
/**/
DECLARE @cuUnitIsDecimal BIT
SET @cuUnitIsDecimal = (SELECT TOP 1 [IsDecimal]
						FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnits] 
						WHERE [CurrencyUnitID] = @ordCurrencyUnitId)

/*RZ14030305- Add ordCurrencyUnitIdI and amounts*/
IF(ISNULL(@ordCurrencyUnitIdI,0) <> 0 AND ISNULL(@ordCurrencyUnitId,0) <> 0 )
BEGIN
/**/
	IF(ISNULL(@ordCurrencyUnitIdI,0) <> ISNULL(@ordCurrencyUnitId,0))
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @ordCurrencyUnitIRate = (SELECT TOP 1 [CurrencyRate]
									FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnitRate] CUR
									WHERE CUR.[DestCurrencyUnitId] = @ordCurrencyUnitIdI
									AND CUR.[SrcCurrencyUnitId] = @ordCurrencyUnitId
									ORDER BY [RateDate] DESC)
	/**/
	END
/**/
END
/*RZ14030305- End*/
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
IF(ISNULL(@InsertedOldOrderID,0) > 0 )
BEGIN
/**/
	SELECT -1 AS 'Result'
	SET @InsertedOrderID = @InsertedOldOrderID
	SEt @InsertedOrderNumber = (SELECT TOP(1) ordNumber FROM [dbo].[WSOrder]  WHERE OrderId = @InsertedOldOrderID)
/**/
END
ELSE IF(ISNULL(@BuyOrderId,0) > 0 )
BEGIN
/**/
	SELECT -2 AS 'Result'
	SET @InsertedOrderID = @BuyOrderId
	SEt @InsertedOrderNumber = (SELECT TOP(1) bordNumber FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrder]  WHERE BuyOrderId = @BuyOrderId)
/**/
END
ELSE IF(ISNULL(@DocItemList,'') <> '' AND (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM OPENJSON(@DocItemList)) = 0)
BEGIN
/**/
	SELECT -2 AS 'Result'
	SET @InsertedOrderID = 0
	SEt @InsertedOrderNumber = 0
/**/
END
ELSE 
BEGIN
/**/
	/*----------------------------------------------*/
	DECLARE @ErrorNumber INT, @ErrorFrom VARCHAR(50)
	BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
	BEGIN TRAN
	/*------------------- tran1 --------------------*/
	INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrder]
        ([OrderOprID]
        ,[PreOrderId]
        ,[PreOrderVerifierId]
        ,[ordTitle]
        ,[ordNumber]
        ,[ordDate]

        ,[ordDescriptionII]        
        ,[ordPrintFormatId]
        ,[ordRLogoId]

        ,[ordSigningI]

        ,[ordAccountShabaNumberII]
